   214	   175	    // Guard: an approve while NOT awaiting is refused (state unchanged).
   215	   176	    assert.equal(awaiting.status, 'running', 'approve while running is a no-op (still running)');
   216	   177	    assert.ok(!awaiting.smokeApproval, 'no approval recorded when not awaiting');
   217	   178	
   218	   179	    const waiting = applyLoopEvent(s0, { type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT });
   219	   180	    assert.equal(waiting.status, 'awaiting-smoke-approval');
   220	   181	    // The caller stamps the verify result on the event (verified === true).
   221	   182	    const approved = applyLoopEvent(waiting, { type: LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE, token: 'smoke-tok-1', verified: true });
   222	   183	    assert.equal(approved.status, 'running', 'a VERIFIED approval moves back to running');
   223	   184	    assert.ok(approved.smokeApproval, 'the approval is recorded in state');
   224	   185	    assert.equal(approved.smokeApproval.token, 'smoke-tok-1');
   225	   186	  });

   545	    63	    22	//   applyLoopEvent(state, ev)- pure reducer (the transitions)
   546	    64	    23	//   writeLoopState(root, state, io)   - atomic snapshot write
   547	    65	    24	//   appendLoopEvent(root, ev, io)     - append a journal event, returns its seq
   548	    66	    25	//   loadLoopState(root, io)  - {state, warnings}: snapshot + torn-tolerant replay
   549	    67	    26	// (smoke-approval token functions are pinned in loop-smoke-token.test.mjs.)
   550	    68	    27	//
   551	    69	    28	// RED MECHANISM: dynamic-import-with-catch + an M() guard, so each test reds on
   552	    70	    29	// a MEANINGFUL assertion ("module must load") rather than a bare import crash.
   553	    71	    30	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   554	    72	    31	
   555	    73	    32	let mod = /** @type {any} */ (null);
   556	    74	    33	let importError = /** @type {any} */ (null);
   557	    75	    34	try {
   558	    76	    35	  mod = await import('../../core/src/loop/state.mjs');
   559	    77	    36	} catch (e) {
   560	    78	    37	  importError = e;
   561	    79	    38	}
   562	    80	    39	function M() {
   563	    81	    40	  assert.ok(mod, `core/src/loop/state.mjs must load (import error: ${importError?.message ?? 'none'})`);
   564	    82	    41	  return mod;
   565	    83	    42	}
   566	    84	    43	
   567	    85	    44	const SPEC = () => defaultLoopSpec('Ship the widget');
   568	    86	    45	// A steady io: fixed clock/identity so init is deterministic across two builds.
   569	    87	    46	const io = (over = {}) => makeIo({ now: '2026-07-19T00:00:00.000Z', host: 'host-A', pid: 4242, startTime: 111000, fencingSeed: 1, ...over });
   570	    88	    47	
   571	    89	    48	// Fold events through the reducer from a starting state.
   572	    90	    49	function fold(applyLoopEvent, state, events) {
   573	    91	    50	  return events.reduce((s, e) => applyLoopEvent(s, e), state);
   574	    92	    51	}
   575	    93	    52	
   576	    94	    53	// ===========================================================================

   621	   139	    98	  it('phase-advance moves to the next phase; advancing past the last phase is done', () => {
   622	   140	    99	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   623	   141	   100	    const spec = SPEC();
   624	   142	   101	    const s0 = initLoopState(spec, io());
   625	   143	   102	    const s1 = applyLoopEvent(s0, { type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE });
   626	   144	   103	    assert.equal(s1.phaseIndex, 1);
   627	   145	   104	    assert.equal(s1.status, 'running');
   628	   146	   105	    // Advance through every remaining phase -> done.
   629	   147	   106	    const end = fold(applyLoopEvent, s0, spec.phases.map(() => ({ type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE })));
   630	   148	   107	    assert.equal(end.status, 'done', 'completing the last phase transitions to done');
   631	   149	   108	  });

   633	   151	   110	  it('gate-iteration increments a NAMED gate counter with its verdict', () => {

   640	   158	   117	    assert.equal(s2.status, 'running');
   641	   159	   118	  });
   642	   160	   119	
   643	   161	   120	  it('the 5-cap is a hard invariant: a 6th iteration is REFUSED, the run escalates, never a 6th count', () => {
   644	   162	   121	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   645	   163	   122	    const s0 = initLoopState(SPEC(), io());
   646	   164	   123	    let s = s0;
   647	   165	   124	    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i += 1) s = applyLoopEvent(s, { type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: 'gate-2-a', verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   648	   166	   125	    assert.equal(s.iterations['gate-2-a'], 5, 'five iterations are allowed');
   649	   167	   126	    assert.equal(s.status, 'running');
   650	   168	   127	
   651	   169	   128	    const sixth = applyLoopEvent(s, { type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: 'gate-2-a', verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   652	   170	   129	    assert.equal(sixth.status, 'escalated', 'a 6th attempt escalates instead of running');
   653	   171	   130	    assert.equal(sixth.iterations['gate-2-a'], 5, 'the counter never reaches 6');
   654	   172	   131	    assert.ok(sixth.escalation, 'an escalation record is written');
   655	   173	   132	    assert.equal(sixth.escalation.gate, 'gate-2-a', 'the record names the gate');
   656	   174	   133	    assert.equal(sixth.escalation.iteration, 5, 'the record names the iteration count');
   657	   175	   134	  });

   690	   208	   167	describe('loadLoopState — snapshot + journal replay reproduces the in-memory sequence', () => {
   691	   209	   168	  it('write base snapshot + append events, then reload == the folded in-memory state', async () => {
   692	   210	   169	    const { initLoopState, applyLoopEvent, writeLoopState, appendLoopEvent, loadLoopState, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   693	   211	   170	    const spec = SPEC();
   694	   212	   171	    const events = [
   695	   213	   172	      { type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE },
   696	   214	   173	      { type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: 'gate-1', verdict: 'APPROVED' },
   697	   215	   174	      { type: LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE },
   698	   216	   175	    ];
   699	   217	   176	
   700	   218	   177	    const setup = io();
   701	   219	   178	    const base = initLoopState(spec, setup);
   702	   220	   179	    await writeLoopState('/repo', base, setup);
   703	   221	   180	    for (const e of events) await appendLoopEvent('/repo', e, setup);
   704	   222	   181	
   705	   223	   182	    const reloaded = (await loadLoopState('/repo', setup)).state;
   706	   224	   183	    const inMemory = fold(applyLoopEvent, base, events);
   707	   225	   184	
   708	   226	   185	    assert.equal(reloaded.status, inMemory.status, 'status matches the in-memory fold');
   709	   227	   186	    assert.equal(reloaded.phaseIndex, inMemory.phaseIndex, 'phase index matches');
   710	   228	   187	    assert.deepEqual(reloaded.iterations, inMemory.iterations, 'gate counters match');
   711	   229	   188	  });
   712	   230	   189	
   713	   231	   190	  it('tolerates a torn final journal line (crash mid-append) without throwing', async () => {
   714	   232	   191	    const { initLoopState, writeLoopState, appendLoopEvent, loadLoopState, loopPaths, LOOP_EVENT } = M();
   715	   233	   192	    const setup = io();
   716	   234	   193	    const base = initLoopState(SPEC(), setup);
   717	   235	   194	    await writeLoopState('/repo', base, setup);
   718	   236	   195	    await appendLoopEvent('/repo', { type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: 'gate-1', verdict: 'APPROVED' }, setup);
   719	   237	   196	    // Simulate a crash mid-append: a truncated trailing line.
   720	   238	   197	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   721	   239	   198	    setup.fs.appendFileSync(p.journal, '{"type":"gate-iter'); // torn JSON, no newline
   722	   240	   199	
   723	   241	   200	    let res;
   724	   242	   201	    assert.doesNotThrow(() => { res = undefined; });
   725	   243	   202	    res = await loadLoopState('/repo', setup);
   726	   244	   203	    assert.equal(res.state.iterations['gate-1'], 1, 'the one complete event still applies; the torn tail is ignored');
   727	   245	   204	  });
   728	   246	   205	
   729	   247	   206	  it('COMPLETED-WORK IDEMPOTENCY: replaying the same gate-iteration event does NOT double-increment', async () => {
   730	   248	   207	    const { initLoopState, writeLoopState, loadLoopState, loopPaths } = M();
   731	   249	   208	    const setup = io();
   732	   250	   209	    const base = initLoopState(SPEC(), setup);
   733	   251	   210	    await writeLoopState('/repo', base, setup); // snapshot journalSeq 0
   734	   252	   211	    // Two journal lines carrying the SAME seq/dedupeKey — a duplicate replay of one
   735	   253	   212	    // completed iteration (supervisor-death recovery must not re-run it).
   736	   254	   213	    const p = loopPaths('/repo');
   737	   255	   214	    const dup = { seq: 1, ts: '2026-07-19T00:00:01.000Z', type: 'gate-iteration', gate: 'gate-1', verdict: 'APPROVED', dedupeKey: 'k1' };
   738	   256	   215	    setup.fs.appendFileSync(p.journal, JSON.stringify(dup) + '\n' + JSON.stringify(dup) + '\n');
   739	   257	   216	
   740	   258	   217	    const reloaded = (await loadLoopState('/repo', setup)).state;
   741	   259	   218	    assert.equal(reloaded.iterations['gate-1'], 1, 'the completed iteration is applied exactly once on replay (seq-skip dedup)');
   742	   260	   219	  });
   743	   261	   220	});

   861	   379	    98	    assert.ok(r.driftedInputs.some((/** @type {string} */ n) => /child|assignment/i.test(n)), 'child assignment drift named');
   862	   380	    99	  });
   863	   381	   100	});
   864	   382	
   865	   383	exec
   866	   384	/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md' in /Users/saksham/baton
   867	   385	 succeeded in 0ms:
   868	   386	     1	# Goal-loop + dual-worktree pipeline (Layers 2+3, v1 plan)
   869	   387	     2

   876	   394	     9	passed, a 40-finding surface audit folded, and the live e2e handoff loop
   877	   395	    10	verified through the real installed plugin. Layers 2 and 3 automate the
   878	   396	    11	development methodology itself on top of that foundation: a goal-driven loop
   879	   397	    12	with review gates (Layer 2) and a dual-worktree adversarial write/review
   880	   398	    13	pipeline (Layer 3).
   881	   399	    14	
   882	   400	    15	## Locked decisions (Saksham, 2026-07-18)
   883	   401	    16	
   884	   402	    17	1. **Packaging**: inside the baton repo — new commands `baton loop` and
   885	   403	    18	   `baton pipeline`, sharing the role matrix, resolver, doctor, and
   886	   404	    19	   checkpoint/handoff machinery.
   887	   405	    20	2. **Layer 3 swap cadence**: per subtask — writer and reviewer swap seats
   888	   406	    21	   every subtask; the planner role plans and the merger role performs the
   889	   407	    22	   final adversarial merge (concrete chains live in `baton.config.json`).
   890	   408	    23	3. **Layer 2 smoke gate**: one staged gate after planning — the loop must
   891	   409	    24	   produce a minimal end-to-end slice (build + run + one real assertion) and
   892	   410	    25	   get explicit approval before the full run; the 5-iteration cap governs
   893	   411	    26	   every gate.
   894	   412	    27	4. **Driver**: a supervisor CLI — spawns headless role children
   895	   413	    28	   (`claude -p` / `codex exec`), parses verdicts, and invokes baton handoff
   896	   414	    29	   automatically on limit death. Survives the operator's session ending.
   897	   415	    30	
   898	   416	    31	## Design
   899	   417	    32	
   900	   418	    33	### Loop spec
   901	   419	    34	
   902	   420	    35	`loop.json` at repo root (schema `baton/loop@1`), scaffolded by
   903	   421	    36	`baton loop init "<goal>"`:
   904	   422	    37	
   905	   423	    38	- `goal`, `constraints[]` (falsifiable acceptance criteria — the loop's exit
   906	   424	    39	  condition), `smoke` ({cmd, expect} — the staged-gate slice definition),
   907	   425	    40	- `phases` — an ordered list of `{id, role}` entries: `id` is a free label
   908	   426	    41	  for progress display; `role` MUST be a matrix role ID (validation fails on
   909	   427	    42	  any unknown role before a child spawns). Default:
   910	   428	    43	  plan(`planner`) → gate-1(`plan-reviewer`) → smoke — itself a TDD mini-cycle
   911	   429	    44	  per the repo contract: smoke-tests(`test-author`) →
   912	   430	    45	  smoke-verify(`test-verifier`) → smoke-build(`implementer`) → the human
   913	   431	    46	  smoke gate — → per-subtask cycle (`test-author` → `test-verifier` →
   914	   432	    47	  `implementer` → `subtask-reviewer`) → gate-2(`final-reviewer-a`,
   915	   433	    48	  `final-reviewer-b`) → done,
   916	   434	    49	- `budgets` ({iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin, maxChildrenPerPhase}),
   917	   435	    50	- roles resolve through the existing `baton.config.json` matrix — never
   918	   436	    51	  hardcoded models.
   919	   437	    52	
   920	   438	    53	### Role-matrix additions (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 1)
   921	   439	    54	
   922	   440	    55	The loop references roles by ID only; every referenced role must exist in the
   923	   441	    56	matrix or `loop.json` validation fails (`unknown role '<id>'`, exit 2, before
   924	   442	    57	any child spawns). Four roles are ADDED to `baton.config.json` +
   925	   443	    58	`templates/baton.config.json.tpl` in the first implementation task:
   926	   444	    59	
   927	   445	    60	- `subtask-reviewer` — per-subtask diff review inside the loop cycle.
   928	   446	    61	- `merger` — the only role that writes main in pipeline mode; adversarial
   929	   447	    62	  re-check before merge.
   930	   448	    63	- `worker-a`, `worker-b` — the two pipeline seats. Subtask N assigns
   931	   449	    64	  writer = seat (N mod 2), reviewer = the other seat; the SEATS swap, and each
   932	   450	    65	  seat's concrete platform/model comes from its configured chain via the
   933	   451	    66	  resolver. No vendor or model name appears in this plan, child prompts, or
   934	   452	    67	  loop code — chains live in config only.
   935	   453	    68	
   936	   454	    69	### `baton loop` (Layer 2)
   937	   455	    70	
   938	   456	    71	- `baton loop init <goal>` — scaffold spec + plan template; idempotent,
   939	   457	    72	  dry-run-able, same two-phase plan/apply discipline as `baton init`.
   940	   458	    73	- `baton loop run [--approve-smoke <token>]` — the state machine. State lives
   941	   459	    74	  in gitignored `.handoff/loop/state.json` + `journal.ndjson` (same atomic
   942	   460	    75	  write + journal replay machinery as the bundle; loop position is also
   943	   461	    76	  checkpointed into the handoff bundle so a limit death carries it).
   944	   462	    77	
   945	   463	    78	### Root + ownership discipline (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 2)
   946	   464	    79	
   947	   465	    80	One authoritative root: the MAIN repo root where `loop.json` lives. The
   948	   466	    81	supervisor is the SOLE writer of `.handoff/loop/` and of the main root's
   949	   467	    82	handoff bundle; every supervisor-driven baton invocation passes explicit
   950	   468	    83	`--root <mainRoot>` and a supervisor-owned stable session hint
   951	   469	    84	(`--session loop-<runId>` — a NEW flag added to `checkpoint`; `receive`
   952	   470	    85	already has it), so the bundle has exactly one stable owner.
   953	   471	    86	
   954	   472	    87	**Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3,
   955	   473	    88	finding 1)** — children never touch OR read any bundle, by construction: the
   956	   474	    89	supervisor sets `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1` in every child's environment, and
   957	   475	    90	EVERY baton hook command — the hook entrypoints, `baton checkpoint`, and
   958	   476	    91	`baton session-start` (which would otherwise read the bundle and inject
   959	   477	    92	pending-handoff resume context into the child) — quietly no-ops (exit 0,
   960	   478	    93	empty output) when it is set. This covers
   961	   479	    94	Layer-2 children running in the MAIN root (where root discovery would
   962	   480	    95	otherwise find the supervisor-owned bundle and the foreign-session guard
   963	   481	    96	would reject their stable hints) and pipeline children in worktrees alike;
   964	   482	    97	narrative checkpointing for supervised work is the supervisor's job, driven
   965	   483	    98	by parsed child results. Contract tests: (a) Layer-2 — two concurrent
   966	   484	    99	main-root children with distinct stable session hints and live hooks →
   967	   485	   100	zero bundle mutations, zero foreign-session rejections, supervisor
   968	   486	   101	`--session` checkpoints land; (b) pipeline — same from two worktrees while
   969	   487	   102	the supervisor writes loop state.
   970	   488	   103	- Each role invocation is a headless child under the supervisor:
   971	   489	   104	  - claude-code roles: `claude -p "<role prompt>" --permission-mode
   972	   490	   105	    acceptEdits --allowedTools ...` (allowlist per role: reviewers read-only).
   973	   491	   106	  - codex roles: `codex exec -C <root> -s <sandbox> --model <m> -c
   974	   492	   107	    model_reasoning_effort=<e> "<prompt>" </dev/null` (stdin closed — the
   975	   493	   108	    stdin-hang class is a known field failure).
   976	   494	   109	  - Verdict contract: children end with the structured tail
   977	   495	   110	    (`VERDICT: APPROVED|APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|BLOCKED` + FINDINGS) already used
   978	   496	   111	    by every review gate; the supervisor parses exactly that.
   979	   497	   112	
   980	   498	   113	### Child supervision contract (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 7)
   981	   499	   114	
   982	   500	   115	`children.mjs` owns the full child lifecycle, not just argv assembly:
   983	   501	   116	
   984	   502	   117	- Spawn detached in its OWN process group; stdin closed at spawn (the
   985	   503	   118	  codex-exec stdin-hang class); per-role timeout → SIGTERM to the group, 10 s
   986	   504	   119	  grace, then SIGKILL to the group (no zombie grandchildren; reap verified in
   987	   505	   120	  tests with a child that forks a sleeper).
   988	   506	   121	- Full child output streams to `.handoff/loop/children/<child-id>.log` with a
   989	   507	   122	  byte cap (head preserved, middle truncated with a marker — the verdict tail
   990	   508	   123	  is at the END so the tail is never truncated).
   991	   509	   124	- **Verdict parsing is region-bounded (prompt-echo defense)**: only the
   992	   510	   125	  child's FINAL message region is parsed — claude-code children run with a
   993	   511	   126	  structured output format and the parser reads the result field; codex
   994	   512	   127	  children are parsed strictly AFTER the last `tokens used` marker. The
   995	   513	   128	  prompt region (which quotes `VERDICT:` in its instructions) is never
   996	   514	   129	  scanned. Multiple tails in the final region → the LAST one wins.
   997	   515	   130	- An unparseable, absent, or truncated verdict is BLOCKED-with-escalation,
   998	   516	   131	  never APPROVED; a child that exits after making edits but before a verdict
   999	   517	   132	  parks the subtask with its worktree preserved for inspection.
  1000	   518	   133	- A generic nonzero exit classifies as `other-error` (existing classifier
  1001	   519	   134	  rule) → the loop retries once, then parks; it NEVER triggers limit failover
  1002	   520	   135	  without limit-specific evidence.
  1003	   521	   136	- Fixture-driven tests: prompt echo, multiple verdict tails, truncated
  1004	   522	   137	  transcript, timeout kill/reap, zombie grandchild, non-limit nonzero exit.
  1005	   523	   138	
  1006	   524	   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
  1007	   525	   140	
  1008	   526	   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
  1009	   527	   142	
  1010	   528	   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
  1011	   529	   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
  1012	   530	   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
  1013	   531	   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
  1014	   532	   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
  1015	   533	   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
  1016	   534	   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
  1017	   535	   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
  1018	   536	   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
  1019	   537	   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
  1020	   538	   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
  1021	   539	   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
  1022	   540	   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
  1023	   541	   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  1024	   542	   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
  1025	   543	   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
  1026	   544	   159	
  1027	   545	   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
  1028	   546	   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
  1029	   547	   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
  1030	   548	   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
  1031	   549	   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
  1032	   550	   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
  1033	   551	   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
  1034	   552	   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
  1035	   553	   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
  1036	   554	   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
  1037	   555	   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
  1038	   556	   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
  1039	   557	   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
  1040	   558	   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
  1041	   559	   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the
  1042	   560	   175	  process group and freeze the transcript; (2) classify via `baton detect`;
  1043	   561	   176	  non-limit → the retry/park path above. On a limit classification:
  1044	   562	   177	  (3) checkpoint the loop position INTO THE BUNDLE (supervisor
  1045	   563	   178	  `checkpoint --session loop-<runId>`) so the seal carries it and the receipt
  1046	   564	   179	  token binds it; (4) seal via `finalize --reason-class usage-limit --root
  1047	   565	   180	  <mainRoot>` (an already-dead/unsealed bundle takes the degraded-open path
  1048	   566	   181	  receive already supports); (5) refresh probes; (6) `receive --prepare` then
  1049	   567	   182	  `--commit` back-to-back with IDENTICAL intake flags, the supervisor session
  1050	   568	   183	  hint, and the same explicit root, with NO intervening writes; (7) resolver
  1051	   569	   184	  output (with the dead entry avoided) picks the relaunch platform+model;
  1052	   570	   185	  (8) relaunch the child with the resume prompt. A stale-token rejection
  1053	   571	   186	  triggers ONE automatic re-prepare/commit retry; a second failure parks.
  1054	   572	   187	  Between prepare and commit the failover holds the loop transaction lock and
  1055	   573	   188	  writes NOTHING — `.handoff/loop/` writes are not token-bound inputs, so
  1056	   574	   189	  this is enforced by construction and ASSERTED in tests (fs spy: zero
  1057	   575	   190	  `.handoff/loop/` writes inside the window), not assumed. Drift-rejection
  1058	   576	   191	  tests inject the inputs the token actually binds: git change and probe
  1059	   577	   192	  refresh between prepare and commit → stale-token → one retry path. Plus:
  1060	   578	   193	  open-bundle (unsealed) limit death; the full seal→receive→relaunch happy
  1061	   579	   194	  path.
  1062	   580	   195	- **Model-level failover (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 4)**: the resolver
  1063	   581	   196	  gains entry-level avoidance — `avoidEntries: [{platform, model}]` alongside
  1064	   582	   197	  the existing platform-level `avoid[]` — and the classifier gains a
  1065	   583	   198	  `model-unavailable` signature class (distinct from usage-limit; seeded with
  1066	   584	   199	  the verified account-tier rejection string). A model rejected at spawn adds
  1067	   585	   200	  its {platform, model} entry to avoidEntries and re-resolves: other models
  1068	   586	   201	  on the SAME platform remain selectable. Named test: first chain entry
  1069	   587	   202	  rejected as model-unavailable → the next entry on the same platform is
  1070	   588	   203	  selected, with no platform-wide avoidance.
  1071	   589	   204	  All platforms/entries exhausted → the loop parks with a resume-at hint
  1072	   590	   205	  instead of dying.
  1073	   591	   206	- **Caps**: every gate loops at most 5 iterations; on exhaustion the loop
  1074	   592	   207	  parks and escalates (writes `.handoff/loop/ESCALATION.md`, notifies) — never
  1075	   593	   208	  a 6th run.
  1076	   594	   209	
  1077	   595	   210	### `baton pipeline` (Layer 3 — a loop preset)
  1078	   596	   211	
  1079	   597	   212	- `baton pipeline run` = `loop run` with the dual-worktree subtask cycle:
  1080	   598	   213	  - Two persistent worktrees `.worktrees/wt-a`, `.worktrees/wt-b`
  1081	   599	   214	    (`git worktree add`); `pipeline init` ensure-lines `.worktrees/` into
  1082	   600	   215	    `.gitignore` (same idempotent machinery as the `.handoff/` line).
  1083	   601	   216	  - Subtask N: the writer seat implements on a namespaced branch
  1084	   602	   217	    (`baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N`) in its worktree; the reviewer seat reviews
  1085	   603	   218	    the diff from the OTHER worktree (fresh checkout, fresh context). Seats
  1086	   604	   219	    swap every subtask: worker-a writes / worker-b reviews → worker-b writes /
  1087	   605	   220	    worker-a reviews → … (concrete models per seat come from the role
  1088	   606	   221	    matrix — see Role-matrix additions).
  1089	   607	   222	  - Verdict flow: APPROVED → the merger role adversarially re-checks and
  1090	   608	   223	    merges into main (the ONLY writer to main); BLOCKED → writer fixes,
  1091	   609	   224	    ≤5 iterations, then escalate. After each merge both worktrees
  1092	   610	   225	    fast-forward.
  1093	   611	   226	
  1094	   612	   227	  **Worktree transaction layer (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 5)** —
  1095	   613	   228	  `worktrees.mjs` treats every git operation as a guarded transaction:
  1096	   614	   229	  - Branch namespace `baton/wt-*/…` is loop-owned; anything else is never
  1097	   615	   230	    touched.
  1098	   616	   231	  - Preflight before each child: worktree exists in `git worktree list`,
  1099	   617	   232	    index clean, HEAD on the expected branch — a dirty or drifted worktree
  1100	   618	   233	    parks the subtask instead of spawning.
  1101	   619	   234	  - Postflight after each child: HEAD still on the expected branch, main
  1102	   620	   235	    unmoved by the child — violation parks with the evidence.
  1103	   621	   236	  - Merges run under a supervisor-held merge lock; worktree sync after merge
  1104	   622	   237	    is ff-only (a non-ff sync is a corruption signal → park); merge conflicts
  1105	   623	   238	    park + escalate, never auto-resolved.
  1106	   624	   239	  - Stale metadata self-heals: a raw-deleted worktree directory is detected
  1107	   625	   240	    (`git worktree list` vs fs), pruned (`git worktree prune`), and re-added;
  1108	   626	   241	    teardown uses `git worktree remove` + branch deletion so acceptance
  1109	   627	   242	    constraint 5 (no residue) holds.
  1110	   628	   243	  - Adversarial tests: stale branch from a previous run, dirty worker,
  1111	   629	   244	    main moved mid-subtask, merge conflict, raw-deleted `.worktrees/`,
  1112	   630	   245	    untracked `.worktrees/` (missing gitignore line → doctor check).
  1113	   631	   246	
  1114	   632	   247	  **Attribution enforcement for child commits (Gate-1 iteration 1,
  1115	   633	   248	  finding 6)** — the invariant is enforced, not assumed:
  1116	   634	   249	  - The supervisor sets `GIT_AUTHOR_NAME/EMAIL` and `GIT_COMMITTER_NAME/EMAIL`
  1117	   635	   250	    (sole author identity) in EVERY child's environment.
  1118	   636	   251	  - Every merge is gated on an attribution scan over the exact range
  1119	   637	   252	    `main..baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N` — author/committer identity plus the
  1120	   638	   253	    forbidden-trailer patterns (the existing doctor patterns, applied to the
  1121	   639	   254	    full range, not a last-50 window). A violation blocks the merge and
  1122	   640	   255	    parks.
  1123	   641	   256	  - The existing doctor 50-commit scan remains the repo-wide backstop.
  1124	   642	   257	
  1125	   643	   258	### Module layout (all under `core/src/loop/`)
  1126	   644	   259	
  1127	   645	   260	`spec.mjs` (load/validate loop.json incl. role-existence check) · `state.mjs`
  1128	   646	   261	(state machine + journal + smoke-approval token) · `children.mjs` (full child
  1129	   647	   262	lifecycle: spawn/process-group/timeout/log-cap, region-bounded verdict parse,
  1130	   648	   263	detect-on-exit) · `failover.mjs` (the limit-death transaction) ·
  1131	   649	   264	`worktrees.mjs` (guarded git transactions, branch discipline, attribution
  1132	   650	   265	range scan) · `commands/loop.mjs`, `commands/pipeline.mjs`. Plus three Layer-1
  1133	   651	   266	extensions: `roles/resolve.mjs` gains `avoidEntries[]` (entry-level
  1134	   652	   267	avoidance, backward-compatible — omitted means current behavior); the
  1135	   653	   268	signature table gains the `model-unavailable` class; `checkpoint` gains a
  1136	   654	   269	`--session <hint>` flag (supervisor identity), and the
  1137	   655	   270	`BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD` no-op guard lands in the hook entrypoints,
  1138	   656	   271	`checkpoint`, AND `session-start` (every hook-invoked command).
  1139	   657	   272	
  1140	   658	   273	### Acceptance constraints (the loop's exit condition)
  1141	   659	   274	
  1142	   660	   275	1. On a fixture repo, `baton loop run` drives a toy goal end-to-end headlessly:
  1143	   661	   276	   plan → gate-1 → smoke slice AS A TDD MINI-CYCLE (failing smoke assertion
  1144	   662	   277	   authored and verifier-approved BEFORE the slice implementation — order
  1145	   663	   278	   asserted from the journal) → parked for approval → (approved) → 2 subtasks
  1146	   664	   279	   TDD → gate-2 → done, with review artifacts under `reviews/` in the fixture.
  1147	   665	   280	   Layer-2 children run in the main root with live hooks and neither mutate
  1148	   666	   281	   NOR read the supervisor-owned bundle: under `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1`,
  1149	   667	   282	   hook commands on all three platforms produce zero bundle writes AND zero
  1150	   668	   283	   session-start resume context (both asserted).
  1151	   669	   284	2. Simulated limit death (child exits with a verbatim limit banner) mid-subtask
  1152	   670	   285	   → the loop seals, remaps the role off the dead platform, resumes on the
  1153	   671	   286	   other harness, and completes the subtask. Chained A→B→A tested. Separately:
  1154	   672	   287	   a model-unavailable rejection at spawn selects the NEXT entry on the same
  1155	   673	   288	   platform (no platform-wide avoidance).
  1156	   674	   289	3. `baton pipeline run` completes 2 subtasks with alternating writer/reviewer
  1157	   675	   290	   across the two worktrees; the attribution range scan passes for main AND
  1158	   676	   291	   both worker branch ranges (child commits included) — sole author, zero AI
  1159	   677	   292	   attribution; reviewer children run read-only.
  1160	   678	   293	4. The 5-cap parks + escalates on every gate (unit-forced exhaustion test);
  1161	   679	   294	   no gate ever runs a 6th iteration. Killing the supervisor (and its
  1162	   680	   295	   launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  1163	   681	   296	   journal and continues or parks, with no orphaned children.
  1164	   682	   297	5. Deleting `.handoff/` and `.worktrees/` leaves the fixture repo exactly as
  1165	   683	   298	   a plain git repo (no residue outside gitignored trees).
  1166	   684	   299	6. All existing 878 tests stay green; the new suite covers spec validation,
  1167	   685	   300	   state-machine transitions (unit, injected io), child verdict parsing
  1168	   686	   301	   (fixture transcripts), failover (fake children), and worktree discipline.
  1169	   687	   302	
  1170	   688	   303	### Top risks
  1171	   689	   304	
  1172	   690	   305	1. **Headless permission surfaces** — Claude Code children need permission
  1173	   691	   306	   flags that stay inside the sandboxed repo; reviewers get read-only
  1174	   692	   307	   allowlists. Mitigation: per-role allowlist table + spike task 0.
  1175	   693	   308	2. **Verdict-parse drift** — models sometimes wrap the tail; the
  1176	   694	   309	   region-bounded parser (Child supervision contract) takes the LAST tail in
  1177	   695	   310	   the final-message region only and treats an unparseable one as
  1178	   696	   311	   BLOCKED-with-escalation, never as APPROVED.
  1179	   697	   312	3. **Cost runaway** — per-phase child caps + iteration caps + the smoke gate
  1180	   698	   313	   before any full-run spend; every child's token/duration logged to the loop
  1181	   699	   314	   journal.
  1182	   700	   315	4. **Worktree state divergence** — only the merger writes main; workers are
  1183	   701	   316	   branch-jailed; ff-sync after each merge; `baton pipeline doctor` checks
  1184	   702	   317	   worktree health.
  1185	   703	   318	5. **Cross-platform child API drift** — the spawn contract lives in ONE module
  1186	   704	   319	   (children.mjs) with per-platform fixtures, same seam discipline as
  1187	   705	   320	   normalize.mjs.
  1188	   706	   321	
  1189	   707	   322	## Out of scope for this milestone
  1190	   708	   323	
  1191	   709	   324	Multi-repo loops; parallel subtask fan-out (>1 writer at once); cloud/cron
  1192	   710	   325	driving (the supervisor is local-first; scheduled runs are a later layer);
  1193	   711	   326	LLM-judged smoke verdicts (the smoke gate is a human gate by decision 3).
  1194	   712	   327

  1249	   767	     7	/**

  1253	   771	    11	export function appendEntry(fs, path, obj) {

  1255	   773	    13	}
  1256	   774	    14	
  1257	   775	    15	/**
  1258	   776	    16	 * Read every parseable entry. Never throws.
  1259	   777	    17	 * Warnings: {line, kind: 'torn-tail'|'parse-error'|'seq-order', ...}.
  1260	   778	    18	 * A non-monotonic numeric seq is flagged but the entry is kept — replay and
  1261	   779	    19	 * audit must still see it.
  1262	   780	    20	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
  1263	   781	    21	 * @returns {{entries: any[], warnings: any[]}}
  1264	   782	    22	 */
  1265	   783	    23	export function readAllTolerant(fs, path) {
  1266	   784	    24	  /** @type {any[]} */
  1267	   785	    25	  const entries = [];
  1268	   786	    26	  /** @type {any[]} */
  1269	   787	    27	  const warnings = [];
  1270	   788	    28	
  1271	   789	    29	  let raw;
  1272	   790	    30	  try {
  1273	   791	    31	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  1274	   792	    32	  } catch {
  1275	   793	    33	    return { entries, warnings };
  1276	   794	    34	  }
  1277	   795	    35	  if (raw === '') return { entries, warnings };
  1278	   796	    36	
  1279	   797	    37	  const terminated = raw.endsWith('\n');
  1280	   798	    38	  const lines = raw.split('\n');
  1281	   799	    39	  if (terminated) lines.pop();
  1282	   800	    40	
  1283	   801	    41	  let prevSeq = null;
  1284	   802	    42	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  1285	   803	    43	    const lineNo = i + 1;
  1286	   804	    44	    const isLast = i === lines.length - 1;
  1287	   805	    45	    const torn = isLast && !terminated;
  1288	   806	    46	    let parsed;
  1289	   807	    47	    try {
  1290	   808	    48	      parsed = JSON.parse(lines[i]);
  1291	   809	    49	    } catch {
  1292	   810	    50	      warnings.push(torn ? { line: lineNo, kind: 'torn-tail' } : { line: lineNo, kind: 'parse-error' });
  1293	   811	    51	      continue;
  1294	   812	    52	    }
  1295	   813	    53	    if (typeof parsed?.seq === 'number') {
  1296	   814	    54	      if (prevSeq !== null && parsed.seq <= prevSeq) {
  1297	   815	    55	        warnings.push({ line: lineNo, kind: 'seq-order', prev: prevSeq, seq: parsed.seq });
  1298	   816	    56	      }
  1299	   817	    57	      prevSeq = prevSeq === null ? parsed.seq : Math.max(prevSeq, parsed.seq);
  1300	   818	    58	    }
  1301	   819	    59	    entries.push(parsed);
  1302	   820	    60	  }
  1303	   821	    61	  return { entries, warnings };
  1304	   822	    62	}

  1320	   838	    12	/** @param {string} root */
  1321	   839	    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {
  1322	   840	    14	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
  1323	   841	    15	  return {
  1324	   842	    16	    dir,
  1325	   843	    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,
  1326	   844	    18	    bak: `${dir}/bundle.json.bak`,
  1327	   845	    19	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
  1328	   846	    20	    handoffMd: `${dir}/HANDOFF.md`,
  1329	   847	    21	    historyDir: `${dir}/history`,
  1330	   848	    22	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
  1331	   849	    23	    logDir: `${dir}/log`,
  1332	   850	    24	  };
  1333	   851	    25	}
  1334	   852	    26	
  1335	   853	    27	/** @param {string} iso */
  1336	   854	    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  1337	   855	    29	
  1338	   856	    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
  1339	   857	    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
  1340	   858	    32	
  1341	   859	    33	/**
  1342	   860	    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
  1343	   861	    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
  1344	   862	    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
  1345	   863	    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
  1346	   864	    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
  1347	   865	    39	 */
  1348	   866	    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
  1349	   867	    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
  1350	   868	    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
  1351	   869	    43	  try {
  1352	   870	    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
  1353	   871	    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
  1354	   872	    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
  1355	   873	    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
  1356	   874	    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1357	   875	    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
  1358	   876	    50	        return;
  1359	   877	    51	      }
  1360	   878	    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
  1361	   879	    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
  1362	   880	    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
  1363	   881	    55	
  1364	   882	    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
  1365	   883	    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
  1366	   884	    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
  1367	   885	    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
  1368	   886	    60	        }
  1369	   887	    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1370	   888	    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  1371	   889	    63	        }
  1372	   890	    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1373	   891	    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
  1374	   892	    66	      } else {
  1375	   893	    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  1376	   894	    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
  1377	   895	    69	      }
  1378	   896	    70	    });
  1379	   897	    71	  } catch (err) {
  1380	   898	    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
  1381	   899	    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
  1382	   900	    74	      return;
  1383	   901	    75	    }
  1384	   902	    76	    throw err;
  1385	   903	    77	  }
  1386	   904	    78	}
  1387	   905	    79	
  1388	   906	    80	/**
  1389	   907	    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
  1390	   908	    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
  1391	   909	    83	 */
  1392	   910	    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
  1393	   911	    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
  1394	   912	    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
  1395	   913	    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  1396	   914	    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
  1397	   915	    89	    warnings.push(
  1398	   916	    90	      snap.ok
  1399	   917	    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
  1400	   918	    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
  1401	   919	    93	    );
  1402	   920	    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
  1403	   921	    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
  1404	   922	    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
  1405	   923	    97	      return bak.value;
  1406	   924	    98	    }
  1407	   925	    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
  1408	   926	   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1409	   927	   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
  1410	   928	   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  1411	   929	   103	    }
  1412	   930	   104	    return null;
  1413	   931	   105	  }
  1414	   932	   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1415	   933	   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
  1416	   934	   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  1417	   935	   109	  }
  1418	   936	   110	  return null;
  1419	   937	   111	}
  1420	   938	   112	
  1421	   939	   113	/**
  1422	   940	   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
  1423	   941	   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
  1424	   942	   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
  1425	   943	   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
  1426	   944	   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
  1427	   945	   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  1428	   946	   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
  1429	   947	   121	 */
  1430	   948	   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
  1431	   949	   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  1432	   950	   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1433	   951	   125	  const warnings = [];
  1434	   952	   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  1435	   953	   127	
  1436	   954	   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
  1437	   955	   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
  1438	   956	   130	
  1439	   957	   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
  1440	   958	   132	
  1441	   959	   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
  1442	   960	   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
  1443	   961	   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
  1444	   962	   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
  1445	   963	   137	  }
  1446	   964	   138	
  1447	   965	   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
  1448	   966	   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  1449	   967	   141	
  1450	   968	   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1451	   969	   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
  1452	   970	   144	    for (const w of jw) {
  1453	   971	   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
  1454	   972	   146	    }
  1455	   973	   147	    for (const e of entries) {
  1456	   974	   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
  1457	   975	   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
  1458	   976	   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
  1459	   977	   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
  1460	   978	   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
  1461	   979	   153	        continue;
  1462	   980	   154	      }
  1463	   981	   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
  1464	   982	   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
  1465	   983	   157	    }
  1466	   984	   158	  }
  1467	   985	   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
  1468	   986	   160	}
  1469	   987	   161	
  1470	   988	   162	/**
  1471	   989	   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
  1472	   990	   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
  1473	   991	   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
  1474	   992	   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
  1475	   993	   167	 */
  1476	   994	   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
  1477	   995	   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  1478	   996	   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  1479	   997	   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  1480	   998	   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  1481	   999	   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
  1482	  1000	   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
  1483	  1001	   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
  1484	  1002	   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
  1485	  1003	   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
  1486	  1004	   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
  1487	  1005	   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  1488	  1006	   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
  1489	  1007	   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  1490	  1008	   182	    }
  1491	  1009	   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
  1492	  1010	   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
  1493	  1011	   185	  });
  1494	  1012	   186	}
  1495	  1013	   187	
  1496	  1014	   188	/**
  1497	  1015	   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
  1498	  1016	   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
  1499	  1017	   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
  1500	  1018	   192	 */
  1501	  1019	   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
  1502	  1020	   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
  1503	  1021	   195	}
  1504	  1022	   196	
  1505	  1023	   197	/**
  1506	  1024	   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
  1507	  1025	   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
  1508	  1026	   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
  1509	  1027	   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
  1510	  1028	   202	 */
  1511	  1029	   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
  1512	  1030	   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  1513	  1031	   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
  1514	  1032	   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  1515	  1033	   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  1516	  1034	   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  1517	  1035	   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
  1518	  1036	   210	      let toAppend = entry;
  1519	  1037	   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  1520	  1038	   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  1521	  1039	   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  1522	  1040	   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
  1523	  1041	   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1524	  1042	   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  1525	  1043	   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  1526	  1044	   218	          }
  1527	  1045	   219	        }
  1528	  1046	   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  1529	  1047	   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
  1530	  1048	   222	      }
  1531	  1049	   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
  1532	  1050	   224	      return seq;
  1533	  1051	   225	    }),
  1534	  1052	   226	  );
  1535	  1053	   227	}
  1536	  1054	   228	
  1537	  1055	   229	/**
  1538	  1056	   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
  1539	  1057	   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
  1540	  1058	   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
  1541	  1059	   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  1542	  1060	   234	 */
  1543	  1061	   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
  1544	  1062	   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
  1545	  1063	   237	}
  1546	  1064	   238	
  1547	  1065	   239	/**
  1548	  1066	   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
  1549	  1067	   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
  1550	  1068	   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  1551	  1069	   243	 */
  1552	  1070	   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
  1553	  1071	   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  1554	  1072	   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  1555	  1073	   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  1556	  1074	   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  1557	  1075	   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
  1558	  1076	   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
  1559	  1077	   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  1560	  1078	   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  1561	  1079	   253	
  1562	  1080	   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
  1563	  1081	   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
  1564	  1082	   256	
  1565	  1083	   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
  1566	  1084	   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
  1567	  1085	   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
  1568	  1086	   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  1569	  1087	   261	    } else {
  1570	  1088	   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
  1571	  1089	   263	    }
  1572	  1090	   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  1573	  1091	   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
  1574	  1092	   266	    } else {
  1575	  1093	   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
  1576	  1094	   268	    }
  1577	  1095	   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  1578	  1096	   270	
  1579	  1097	   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
  1580	  1098	   272	    const freezes = io.fs
  1581	  1099	   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
  1582	  1100	   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
  1583	  1101	   275	      .sort();
  1584	  1102	   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
  1585	  1103	   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
  1586	  1104	   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
  1587	  1105	   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
  1588	  1106	   280	        try {
  1589	  1107	   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
  1590	  1108	   282	        } catch {
  1591	  1109	   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
  1592	  1110	   284	        }
  1593	  1111	   285	      }
  1594	  1112	   286	    }
  1595	  1113	   287	
  1596	  1114	   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
  1597	  1115	   289	    return stem;
  1598	  1116	   290	  });
  1599	  1117	   291	}

  1806	  1324	   104	    platform: opts.platform,
  1807	  1325	   105	    sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
  1808	  1326	   106	  };
  1809	  1327	   107	}
  1810	  1328	   108	
  1811	  1329	   109	/**
  1812	  1330	   110	 * Structured receipt token: a fixed-order join of per-input digests. Equality
  1813	  1331	   111	 * still gates commit exactly as the old opaque hash did; the structure only
  1814	  1332	   112	 * buys DIAGNOSIS — a mismatch can name the drifted input(s).
  1815	  1333	   113	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1816	  1334	   114	 */
  1817	  1335	   115	function deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io) {
  1818	  1336	   116	  const inputs = tokenInputs(root, bundle, opts, io);
  1819	  1337	   117	  return `${TOKEN_PREFIX}.` + TOKEN_FIELDS.map(([key]) => dedupeKey(inputs[key] ?? null).slice(0, 10)).join('.');
  1820	  1338	   118	}
  1821	  1339	   119	
  1822	  1340	   120	/**
  1823	  1341	   121	 * Name the inputs whose digests differ between two structured tokens.
  1824	  1342	   122	 * @param {string} presented @param {string} expected @returns {string[]}
  1825	  1343	   123	 */
  1826	  1344	   124	function driftedInputs(presented, expected) {
  1827	  1345	   125	  const a = presented.split('.');
  1828	  1346	   126	  const b = expected.split('.');
  1829	  1347	   127	  if (a[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || b[0] !== TOKEN_PREFIX || a.length !== b.length) return [];
  1830	  1348	   128	  return TOKEN_FIELDS.filter((_, i) => a[i + 1] !== b[i + 1]).map(([, name]) => name);
  1831	  1349	   129	}
  1832	  1350	   130	
  1833	  1351	   131	/**
  1834	  1352	   132	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1835	  1353	   133	 * @returns {string[]} staleness/degradation warnings
  1836	  1354	   134	 */
  1837	  1355	   135	function stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io) {
  1838	  1356	   136	  /** @type {string[]} */
  1839	  1357	   137	  const warnings = [];
  1840	  1358	   138	  if (bundle.handoff.status === 'open') {
  1841	  1359	   139	    warnings.push('source bundle is unsealed (open) — the handoff was never finalized; treating this as a degraded (limit-death) receive');
  1842	  1360	   140	  }
  1843	  1361	   141	  const ref = bundle.handoff.finalizedAt ?? bundle.updatedAt;
  1844	  1362	   142	  const ageMs = Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(ref);
  1845	  1363	   143	  if (Number.isFinite(ageMs) && ageMs > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
  1846	  1364	   144	    warnings.push(`bundle finalized ${Math.round(ageMs / 3_600_000)}h ago (staleness threshold 12h) — re-verify before continuing`);
  1847	  1365	   145	  }
  1848	  1366	   146	  const g = opts.gitSnapshot;
  1849	  1367	   147	  if (g && bundle.git) {
  1850	  1368	   148	    if (bundle.git.headSha !== g.headSha) {
  1851	  1369	   149	      warnings.push(`HEAD moved since capture: bundle recorded ${bundle.git.headSha}, working tree now ${g.headSha}`);
  1852	  1370	   150	    }
  1853	  1371	   151	    if (bundle.git.dirty !== g.dirty) {
  1854	  1372	   152	      warnings.push(`dirty-state mismatch: bundle captured dirty=${bundle.git.dirty}, working tree now dirty=${g.dirty}`);
  1855	  1373	   153	    }
  1856	  1374	   154	  }
  1857	  1375	   155	  return warnings;
  1858	  1376	   156	}
  1859	  1377	   157	
  1860	  1378	   158	/**
  1861	  1379	   159	 * Phase 1 of the receive transaction: validate, resolve roles, render the
  1862	  1380	   160	 * resume prompt, and return a one-time receipt token. Mutates NOTHING — the
  1863	  1381	   161	 * token is self-contained (a fingerprint hash), never a persisted record.
  1864	  1382	   162	 * @param {string} root @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1865	  1383	   163	 * @returns {{token: string, prompt: string, assignments: Record<string, any>, warnings: string[]}}
  1866	  1384	   164	 */
  1867	  1385	   165	export function prepare(root, opts, io) {
  1868	  1386	   166	  const { bundle, warnings: loadWarnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
  1869	  1387	   167	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive`);
  1870	  1388	   168	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
  1871	  1389	   169	
  1872	  1390	   170	  const warnings = [...loadWarnings, ...stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io)];
  1873	  1391	   171	
  1874	  1392	   172	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
  1875	  1393	   173	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
  1876	  1394	   174	  let assignments = {};
  1877	  1395	   175	  if (config) {
  1878	  1396	   176	    // The dead origin comes from the BUNDLE first (its sealed reasonClass and
  1879	  1397	   177	    // recorded origin platform); typed intake only fills the gaps — a user
  1880	  1398	   178	    // should not need to retype the verbatim limit string to keep roles off
  1881	  1399	   179	    // the platform that died (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 4). Confidence gates
  1882	  1400	   180	    // auto-avoidance (gate-2 iter-2 M4): an explicit --reason-class or a sealed
  1883	  1401	   181	    // reasonClass is trusted; a live text classification only auto-avoids at
  1884	  1402	   182	    // medium+ confidence, else it warns and defers to explicit intake.
  1885	  1403	   183	    let reasonClass;
  1886	  1404	   184	    let confidence;
  1887	  1405	   185	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1888	  1406	   186	      reasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
  1889	  1407	   187	      confidence = 'high'; // explicit user intake
  1890	  1408	   188	    } else if (typeof bundle.handoff?.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1891	  1409	   189	      reasonClass = bundle.handoff.reasonClass;
  1892	  1410	   190	      confidence = 'high'; // trusted seal
  1893	  1411	   191	    } else {
  1894	  1412	   192	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
  1895	  1413	   193	      reasonClass = v.class;
  1896	  1414	   194	      confidence = v.confidence;
  1897	  1415	   195	    }
  1898	  1416	   196	    const deadOrigin =
  1899	  1417	   197	      typeof bundle.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : opts.origin;
  1900	  1418	   198	    /** @type {string[]} */
  1901	  1419	   199	    let avoid = [];
  1902	  1420	   200	    if (reasonClass === 'usage-limit') {
  1903	  1421	   201	      if (confidence === 'low') {
  1904	  1422	   202	        warnings.push(
  1905	  1423	   203	          `the switch reason matched a usage-limit heuristic only at LOW confidence — not auto-avoiding ${deadOrigin}; pass --reason-class usage-limit to confirm the failover away from it`,
  1906	  1424	   204	        );
  1907	  1425	   205	      } else {
  1908	  1426	   206	        avoid = [deadOrigin];
  1909	  1427	   207	      }
  1910	  1428	   208	    }
  1911	  1429	   209	    assignments = resolveRoles({ config, to: opts.platform, avoid, nativeOnly: false, probes: opts.probes ?? null }).assignments;
  1912	  1430	   210	  } else {
  1913	  1431	   211	    warnings.push(`role matrix unavailable (${errors.map((e) => e.msg).join('; ')}) — no role table in this prompt`);
  1914	  1432	   212	  }
  1915	  1433	   213	
  1916	  1434	   214	  const prompt = renderResumePrompt({ bundle, assignments, warnings, origin: opts.origin, reason: opts.reason });
  1917	  1435	   215	  return { token: deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io), prompt, assignments, warnings };
  1918	  1436	   216	}
  1919	  1437	   217	
  1920	  1438	   218	/**
  1921	  1439	   219	 * Phase 2: one atomic transition. Re-derives every bound input from current
  1922	  1440	   220	 * disk state + opts and rejects ANY drift (throws, zero mutation). On match:
  1923	  1441	   221	 * writes the received seal, archives it via a 'receive' rotation, then opens a
  1924	  1442	   222	 * fresh writable generation owned by the receiving platform/session.
  1925	  1443	   223	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
  1926	  1444	   224	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
  1927	  1445	   225	 */
  1928	  1446	   226	export function commit(root, token, opts, io) {
  1929	  1447	   227	  // ONE operation-scoped lock across revalidation and every mutation (gate-2
  1930	  1448	   228	  // fix: reviewers a#1/b#11): two competing receivers serialize here, and the
  1931	  1449	   229	  // loser's re-derivation sees the winner's generation/status/receive_log.
  1932	  1450	   230	  return withLock(root, io, (fence) => commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence));
  1933	  1451	   231	}
  1934	  1452	   232	
  1935	  1453	   233	/**
  1936	  1454	   234	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io @param {string} fence
  1937	  1455	   235	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string | null, alreadyCommitted?: boolean}}
  1938	  1456	   236	 */
  1939	  1457	   237	function commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence) {
  1940	  1458	   238	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
  1941	  1459	   239	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive; run receive --prepare first`);
  1942	  1460	   240	  opts = effectiveIntake(bundle, opts);
  1943	  1461	   241	
  1944	  1462	   242	  const expected = deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io);
  1945	  1463	   243	  if (expected !== token) {
  1946	  1464	   244	    // Idempotency first (audit finding: duplicate-receive corruption): if THIS
  1947	  1465	   245	    // exact receipt already landed — its digest is recorded on a receive_log
  1948	  1466	   246	    // entry — the retry is a lost-output re-send, not drift. Report success
  1949	  1467	   247	    // with zero mutation instead of sending the model into a re-prepare +
  1950	  1468	   248	    // re-commit loop that executes a second full receive.
  1951	  1469	   249	    const receipt = dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16);
  1952	  1470	   250	    const landed = Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e?.receipt === receipt);
  1953	  1471	   251	    if (landed) {
  1954	  1472	   252	      return { adopted: false, alreadyCommitted: true, generation: bundle.generation, archivedTo: null };
  1955	  1473	   253	    }
  1956	  1474	   254	    const drifted = driftedInputs(token, expected);
  1957	  1475	   255	    const detail = drifted.length > 0 ? `drifted input(s): ${drifted.join(', ')}` : 'a bound input drifted since prepare';
  1958	  1476	   256	    throw new Error(
  1959	  1477	   257	      `receipt token is stale — ${detail}. Re-prepare and retry, passing the SAME intake flags (--origin/--reason/--reason-class) to both prepare and --commit`,
  1960	  1478	   258	    );
  1961	  1479	   259	  }
  1962	  1480	   260	
  1963	  1481	   261	  const degraded = bundle.handoff.status !== 'sealed';
  1964	  1482	   262	  const receiveEntry = {
  1965	  1483	   263	    origin: opts.origin,
  1966	  1484	   264	    reason: opts.reason,
  1967	  1485	   265	    at: io.now(),
  1968	  1486	   266	    // The receipt digest keys the idempotent-retry detection above.
  1969	  1487	   267	    receipt: dedupeKey(token).slice(0, 16),
  1970	  1488	   268	    ...(degraded ? { degradedSeal: true } : {}),
  1971	  1489	   269	  };
  1972	  1490	   270	
  1973	  1491	   271	  // The degraded seal must NOT persist a LOW-confidence classification (iter-3
  1974	  1492	   272	  // F10): a sealed reasonClass is trusted as high-confidence by the next
  1975	  1493	   273	  // prepare, so laundering a low-confidence heuristic here would silently
  1976	  1494	   274	  // auto-avoid the origin one hop later. Explicit intake is high-confidence and
  1977	  1495	   275	  // seals; a live classification seals ONLY at medium+ confidence, else null
  1978	  1496	   276	  // (the next receive re-classifies the reason text at its true confidence).
  1979	  1497	   277	  let sealedReasonClass = null;
  1980	  1498	   278	  if (degraded) {
  1981	  1499	   279	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
  1982	  1500	   280	      sealedReasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
  1983	  1501	   281	    } else {
  1984	  1502	   282	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
  1985	  1503	   283	      sealedReasonClass = v.confidence === 'low' ? null : v.class;
  1986	  1504	   284	    }
  1987	  1505	   285	  }
  1988	  1506	   286	
  1989	  1507	   287	  const receivedSeal = {
  1990	  1508	   288	    ...bundle,
  1991	  1509	   289	    updatedAt: io.now(),
  1992	  1510	   290	    handoff: {
  1993	  1511	   291	      ...bundle.handoff,
  1994	  1512	   292	      status: 'received',
  1995	  1513	   293	      ...(degraded
  1996	  1514	   294	        ? {
  1997	  1515	   295	            reason: opts.reason,
  1998	  1516	   296	            reasonClass: sealedReasonClass,
  1999	  1517	   297	            finalizedAt: io.now(),
  2000	  1518	   298	          }
  2001	  1519	   299	        : {}),
  2002	  1520	   300	      receive_log: [...bundle.handoff.receive_log, receiveEntry],
  2003	  1521	   301	    },
  2004	  1522	   302	  };
  2005	  1523	   303	
  2006	  1524	   304	  // Freeze order: the received seal is written first so the 'receive' rotation
  2007	  1525	   305	  // archives exactly that state (never the pre-commit bundle). All three
  2008	  1526	   306	  // mutations run under the SAME lock acquisition, fence-checked per write.
  2009	  1527	   307	  writeSnapshotIn(root, receivedSeal, io, fence);
  2010	  1528	   308	  const archivedTo = rotateJournalIn(root, 'receive', io, fence);
  2011	  1529	   309	
  2012	  1530	   310	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
  2013	  1531	   311	  const next = {
  2014	  1532	   312	    ...receivedSeal,
  2015	  1533	   313	    generation: bundle.generation + 1,
  2016	  1534	   314	    origin: {
  2017	  1535	   315	      platform: opts.platform,
  2018	  1536	   316	      model: config?.defaults?.[opts.platform] ?? 'unknown',
  2019	  1537	   317	      sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
  2020	  1538	   318	      unstable: opts.sessionUnstable === true,
  2021	  1539	   319	    },
  2022	  1540	   320	    // The fresh writable generation has NO switch reason yet (iter-4 I3): the
  2023	  1541	   321	    // sealed reason/reasonClass/finalizedAt/toPlatformHint describe the PRIOR
  2024	  1542	   322	    // handoff, not this one. Carrying them forward made the next prepare trust a
  2025	  1543	   323	    // stale class as a high-confidence seal (silently auto-avoiding the healthy
  2026	  1544	   324	    // adopted origin) and poisoned both the 12 h staleness reference and the
  2027	  1545	   325	    // sessionStart limit-hit signal. Keep only receive_log (the audit chain).
  2028	  1546	   326	    handoff: { ...receivedSeal.handoff, status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, finalizedAt: null, toPlatformHint: null },
  2029	  1547	   327	  };
  2030	  1548	   328	  writeSnapshotIn(root, next, io, fence);
  2031	  1549	   329	
  2032	  1550	   330	  return { adopted: true, generation: next.generation, archivedTo };
  2033	  1551	   331	}
  2034	  1552	
  2035	  1553	exec
  2036	  1554	/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba core/src/loop/spec.mjs' in /Users/saksham/baton
  2037	  1555	 succeeded in 0ms:
  2038	  1556	     1	/**
  2039	  1557	     2	 * Loop spec (baton/loop@1) — pure load/validate for the Layer-2 goal loop.
  2040	  1558	     3	 * The spec is committed project config (loop.json at the repo root); run
  2041	  1559	     4	 * state lives elsewhere (.handoff/loop/). Plan:
  2042	  1560	     5	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Loop spec".
  2043	  1561	     6	 */
  2044	  1562	     7	
  2045	  1563	     8	export const LOOP_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop@1';
  2046	  1564	     9	
  2047	  1565	    10	// The 5-cap is a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md §Review gates): a spec
  2048	  1566	    11	// asking for a 6th gate iteration is invalid, not a preference.
  2049	  1567	    12	export const ITERATION_CAP_MAX = 5;
  2050	  1568	    13	
  2051	  1569	    14	const DEFAULT_BUDGETS = Object.freeze({ iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 });
  2052	  1570	    15	
  2053	  1571	    16	/**
  2054	  1572	    17	 * The scaffold `baton loop init` writes. Phase ids are progress labels;
  2055	  1573	    18	 * every role is a matrix role ID resolved via baton.config.json — never a
  2056	  1574	    19	 * model name.
  2057	  1575	    20	 * @param {string} goal
  2058	  1576	    21	 * @returns {any}
  2059	  1577	    22	 */
  2060	  1578	    23	export function defaultLoopSpec(goal) {
  2061	  1579	    24	  return {
  2062	  1580	    25	    schema: LOOP_SCHEMA,
  2063	  1581	    26	    goal,
  2064	  1582	    27	    constraints: [],
  2065	  1583	    28	    smoke: { cmd: null, expect: null },
  2066	  1584	    29	    phases: [

  2068	  1586	    31	      { id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' },
  2069	  1587	    32	      // The smoke slice is itself a TDD mini-cycle (Gate-1 iter-2 finding 2):
  2070	  1588	    33	      // failing smoke assertion → verifier approval → minimal implementation.
  2071	  1589	    34	      { id: 'smoke-tests', role: 'test-author' },
  2072	  1590	    35	      { id: 'smoke-verify', role: 'test-verifier' },
  2073	  1591	    36	      { id: 'smoke-build', role: 'implementer' },
  2074	  1592	    37	      { id: 'subtask-tests', role: 'test-author' },

  2076	  1594	    39	      { id: 'subtask-implement', role: 'implementer' },
  2077	  1595	    40	      { id: 'subtask-review', role: 'subtask-reviewer' },
  2078	  1596	    41	      { id: 'gate-2-a', role: 'final-reviewer-a' },
  2079	  1597	    42	      { id: 'gate-2-b', role: 'final-reviewer-b' },

  2083	  1601	    46	}
  2084	  1602	    47	
  2085	  1603	    48	/**
  2086	  1604	    49	 * Validate a loop spec against the role matrix. Reports EVERY problem at
  2087	  1605	    50	 * once (each error names its offender) so a spec author fixes one round, not
  2088	  1606	    51	 * one field per run. Valid specs are returned normalized: caller-provided
  2089	  1607	    52	 * fields are preserved verbatim; only ABSENT constraints/budgets/smoke are
  2090	  1608	    53	 * filled with defaults.
  2091	  1609	    54	 * @param {any} spec
  2092	  1610	    55	 * @param {any} config the parsed baton.config.json (roles are checked against it)
  2093	  1611	    56	 * @returns {{ok: true, spec: any} | {ok: false, errors: string[]}}
  2094	  1612	    57	 */
  2095	  1613	    58	export function validateLoopSpec(spec, config) {
  2096	  1614	    59	  /** @type {string[]} */
  2097	  1615	    60	  const errors = [];
  2098	  1616	    61	  const s = spec ?? {};
  2099	  1617	    62	
  2100	  1618	    63	  if (s.schema !== LOOP_SCHEMA) errors.push(`schema must be '${LOOP_SCHEMA}' (got ${JSON.stringify(s.schema)})`);
  2101	  1619	    64	  if (typeof s.goal !== 'string' || s.goal.length === 0) errors.push('goal must be a non-empty string');
  2102	  1620	    65	
  2103	  1621	    66	  let constraints = s.constraints;
  2104	  1622	    67	  if (constraints === undefined) constraints = [];
  2105	  1623	    68	  if (!Array.isArray(constraints)) {
  2106	  1624	    69	    errors.push('constraints must be an array of strings');
  2107	  1625	    70	  } else {
  2108	  1626	    71	    constraints.forEach((c, i) => {
  2109	  1627	    72	      if (typeof c !== 'string') errors.push(`constraints[${i}] must be a string (got ${JSON.stringify(c)})`);
  2110	  1628	    73	    });
  2111	  1629	    74	  }
  2112	  1630	    75	
  2113	  1631	    76	  const roles = config?.roles ?? {};
  2114	  1632	    77	  if (!Array.isArray(s.phases) || s.phases.length === 0) {
  2115	  1633	    78	    errors.push('phases must be a non-empty array of {id, role}');
  2116	  1634	    79	  } else {
  2117	  1635	    80	    s.phases.forEach((/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
  2118	  1636	    81	      if (typeof p?.id !== 'string' || p.id.length === 0) errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing an id (a non-empty progress label)`);
  2119	  1637	    82	      if (typeof p?.role !== 'string' || p.role.length === 0) {
  2120	  1638	    83	        errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing a role (a baton.config.json role ID)`);
  2121	  1639	    84	      } else if (!(p.role in roles)) {
  2122	  1640	    85	        errors.push(`phases[${i}]: unknown role '${p.role}' — add it to baton.config.json roles or fix the reference`);
  2123	  1641	    86	      }
  2124	  1642	    87	    });
  2125	  1643	    88	  }
  2126	  1644	    89	
  2127	  1645	    90	  let budgets = s.budgets;
  2128	  1646	    91	  if (budgets === undefined) {
  2129	  1647	    92	    budgets = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS };
  2130	  1648	    93	  } else if (typeof budgets !== 'object' || budgets === null) {
  2131	  1649	    94	    errors.push('budgets must be an object');

  3023	  2541	    15	function normalize(p) {
  3024	  2542	    16	  const str = String(p);
  3025	  2543	    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
  3026	  2544	    18	  const out = [];
  3027	  2545	    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
  3028	  2546	    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
  3029	  2547	    21	    if (part === '..') {
  3030	  2548	    22	      out.pop();
  3031	  2549	    23	      continue;
  3032	  2550	    24	    }
  3033	  2551	    25	    out.push(part);
  3034	  2552	    26	  }
  3035	  2553	    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
  3036	  2554	    28	}

  3038	  2556	    30	function dirname(np) {

  3044	  2562	    36	function basename(np, parent) {
