  3048	  2566	    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
  3049	  2567	    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
  3050	  2568	    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
  3051	  2569	    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
  3052	  2570	    44	  const history = [];
  3053	  2571	    45	  let clock = 0;
  3054	  2572	    46	
  3055	  2573	    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
  3056	  2574	    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
  3057	  2575	    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
  3058	  2576	    50	
  3059	  2577	    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
  3060	  2578	    52	    let cur = '';
  3061	  2579	    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
  3062	  2580	    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
  3063	  2581	    55	      dirs.add(cur);
  3064	  2582	    56	    }
  3065	  2583	    57	  };
  3066	  2584	    58	
  3067	  2585	    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
  3068	  2586	    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
  3069	  2587	    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
  3070	  2588	    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
  3071	  2589	    63	  };
  3072	  2590	    64	
  3073	  2591	    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
  3074	  2592	    66	    const np = normalize(p);
  3075	  2593	    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
  3076	  2594	    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
  3077	  2595	    69	    touch(np);
  3078	  2596	    70	  }
  3079	  2597	    71	
  3080	  2598	    72	  const fs = {
  3081	  2599	    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
  3082	  2600	    74	      const np = normalize(p);
  3083	  2601	    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
  3084	  2602	    76	      return store.get(np);
  3085	  2603	    77	    },
  3086	  2604	    78	
  3087	  2605	    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
  3088	  2606	    80	      const np = normalize(p);
  3089	  2607	    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  3090	  2608	    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
  3091	  2609	    83	      touch(np);
  3092	  2610	    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
  3093	  2611	    85	    },
  3094	  2612	    86	
  3095	  2613	    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
  3096	  2614	    88	      const np = normalize(p);
  3097	  2615	    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  3098	  2616	    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
  3099	  2617	    91	      touch(np);
  3100	  2618	    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
  3101	  2619	    93	    },
  3102	  2620	    94	
  3103	  2621	    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
  3104	  2622	    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
  3105	  2623	    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
  3106	  2624	    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
  3107	  2625	    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  3108	  2626	   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
  3109	  2627	   101	        store.delete(nf);
  3110	  2628	   102	        store.set(nt, content);
  3111	  2629	   103	        touch(nt);
  3112	  2630	   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  3113	  2631	   105	        return;
  3114	  2632	   106	      }
  3115	  2633	   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
  3116	  2634	   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
  3117	  2635	   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
  3118	  2636	   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  3119	  2637	   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
  3120	  2638	   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
  3121	  2639	   113	        dirs.add(nt);
  3122	  2640	   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
  3123	  2641	   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
  3124	  2642	   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
  3125	  2643	   117	            store.delete(k);
  3126	  2644	   118	          }
  3127	  2645	   119	        }
  3128	  2646	   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
  3129	  2647	   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
  3130	  2648	   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
  3131	  2649	   123	            dirs.delete(d);
  3132	  2650	   124	          }
  3133	  2651	   125	        }
  3134	  2652	   126	        touch(nt);
  3135	  2653	   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  3136	  2654	   128	        return;
  3137	  2655	   129	      }
  3138	  2656	   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
  3139	  2657	   131	    },
  3140	  2658	   132	
  3141	  2659	   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
  3142	  2660	   134	      const np = normalize(p);
  3143	  2661	   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
  3144	  2662	   136	        mkdirp(np);
  3145	  2663	   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  3146	  2664	   138	        return undefined;
  3147	  2665	   139	      }
  3148	  2666	   140	      ensureParent(np, 'mkdir');
  3149	  2667	   141	      if (dirs.has(np) || store.has(np)) throw fsError('EEXIST', 'mkdir', np);
  3150	  2668	   142	      dirs.add(np);
  3151	  2669	   143	      record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  3152	  2670	   144	      return undefined;
  3153	  2671	   145	    },
  3154	  2672	   146	
  3155	  2673	   147	    existsSync(p) {
  3156	  2674	   148	      const np = normalize(p);
  3157	  2675	   149	      return store.has(np) || dirs.has(np);
  3158	  2676	   150	    },
  3159	  2677	   151	
  3160	  2678	   152	    readdirSync(p) {
  3161	  2679	   153	      const np = normalize(p);
  3162	  2680	   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {
  3163	  2681	   155	        if (store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOTDIR', 'scandir', np);
  3164	  2682	   156	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'scandir', np);
  3165	  2683	   157	      }
  3166	  2684	   158	      const names = new Set();
  3167	  2685	   159	      const collect = (k) => {
  3168	  2686	   160	        if (k !== np && dirname(k) === np) names.add(basename(k, np));
  3169	  2687	   161	      };
  3170	  2688	   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
  3171	  2689	   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
  3172	  2690	   164	      return [...names];
  3173	  2691	   165	    },
  3174	  2692	   166	
  3175	  2693	   167	    unlinkSync(p) {
  3176	  2694	   168	      const np = normalize(p);
  3177	  2695	   169	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'unlink', np);
  3178	  2696	   170	      store.delete(np);
  3179	  2697	   171	      record('unlinkSync', [np]);
  3180	  2698	   172	    },
  3181	  2699	   173	
  3182	  2700	   174	    rmSync(p, opts = {}) {
  3183	  2701	   175	      const np = normalize(p);
  3184	  2702	   176	      const isFile = store.has(np);
  3185	  2703	   177	      const isDir = dirs.has(np);
  3186	  2704	   178	      if (!isFile && !isDir) {
  3187	  2705	   179	        if (opts.force) return;
  3188	  2706	   180	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rm', np);
  3189	  2707	   181	      }
  3190	  2708	   182	      if (isFile) store.delete(np);
  3191	  2709	   183	      if (isDir) {
  3192	  2710	   184	        const pre = np + '/';
  3193	  2711	   185	        if (!opts.recursive) {
  3194	  2712	   186	          const hasChildren = [...store.keys(), ...dirs].some((k) => k !== np && k.startsWith(pre));
  3195	  2713	   187	          if (hasChildren) throw fsError('ENOTEMPTY', 'rmdir', np);
  3196	  2714	   188	          dirs.delete(np);
  3197	  2715	   189	        } else {
  3198	  2716	   190	          for (const k of [...store.keys()]) if (k === np || k.startsWith(pre)) store.delete(k);
  3199	  2717	   191	          for (const d of [...dirs]) if (d === np || d.startsWith(pre)) dirs.delete(d);
  3200	  2718	   192	        }
  3201	  2719	   193	      }
  3202	  2720	   194	      record('rmSync', [np]);
  3203	  2721	   195	    },
  3204	  2722	   196	
  3205	  2723	   197	    statSync(p) {
  3206	  2724	   198	      const np = normalize(p);
  3207	  2725	   199	      if (store.has(np)) {
  3208	  2726	   200	        const size = Buffer.byteLength(store.get(np));
  3209	  2727	   201	        return { isFile: () => true, isDirectory: () => false, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  3210	  2728	   202	      }
  3211	  2729	   203	      if (dirs.has(np)) {
  3212	  2730	   204	        return { isFile: () => false, isDirectory: () => true, isSymbolicLink: () => false, size: 0, mtimeMs: mtimes.get(np) || 0 };
  3213	  2731	   205	      }
  3214	  2732	   206	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'stat', np);
  3215	  2733	   207	    },
  3216	  2734	   208	
  3217	  2735	   209	    // The fake cannot represent symlinks, so lstat === stat and realpath is
  3218	  2736	   210	    // normalization. Symlink-refusal behavior is proven over the real fs in
  3219	  2737	   211	    // tests/integration/symlink-escape.test.mjs.
  3220	  2738	   212	    lstatSync(p) {
  3221	  2739	   213	      return fs.statSync(p);
  3222	  2740	   214	    },
  3223	  2741	   215	
  3224	  2742	   216	    realpathSync(p) {
  3225	  2743	   217	      const np = normalize(p);
  3226	  2744	   218	      if (!store.has(np) && !dirs.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'realpath', np);
  3227	  2745	   219	      return np;
  3228	  2746	   220	    },
  3229	  2747	   221	
  3230	  2748	   222	    copyFileSync(src, dest) {
  3231	  2749	   223	      const ns = normalize(src);
  3232	  2750	   224	      const nd = normalize(dest);
  3233	  2751	   225	      if (!store.has(ns)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'copyfile', ns);
  3234	  2752	   226	      ensureParent(nd, 'copyfile');
  3235	  2753	   227	      store.set(nd, store.get(ns));
  3236	  2754	   228	      touch(nd);
  3237	  2755	   229	      record('copyFileSync', [ns, nd]);
  3238	  2756	   230	    },
  3239	  2757	   231	  };

  3258	  2776	     9	
  3259	  2777	    10	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3260	  2778	    11	// Contract choices for core/src/receive/txn.mjs (docs/design/core.md §Module APIs
  3261	  2779	    12	// prepare/commit; §Per-module test lists "txn"; plan §Concurrency — the receive
  3262	  2780	    13	// prepare/commit two-phase transaction, receipt-token bindings incl. the
  3263	  2781	    14	// content-sensitive git digest, open->received degraded seal, ownership adoption +
  3264	  2782	    15	// new generation, chained failover, competing receivers, double-commit).
  3265	  2783	    16	//
  3266	  2784	    17	// SIGNATURES (pinned — the task overrides core.md's 3-arg commit):
  3267	  2785	    18	//   prepare(root, opts, io) -> { token, prompt, assignments, warnings }
  3268	  2786	    19	//   commit(root, token, opts, io) -> { adopted, generation, archivedTo }
  3269	  2787	    20	//   Both are SYNCHRONOUS. The plan pins that the receive COMMAND wires
  3270	  2788	    21	//   core/src/git/snapshot.mjs in and passes the result as opts.gitSnapshot; txn
  3271	  2789	    22	//   itself never spawns git — it takes the snapshot as plain data.
  3272	  2790	    23	//
  3273	  2791	    24	//   opts (same shape for prepare and commit):
  3274	  2792	    25	//     { platform,       // destination harness we are resuming ON (e.g. 'codex')
  3275	  2793	    26	//       origin,         // intake: the harness handed off FROM (e.g. 'claude-code')
  3276	  2794	    27	//       reason,         // intake: why the switch happened (free text)
  3277	  2795	    28	//       gitSnapshot,    // the caller-computed git snapshot object (git/snapshot.mjs)
  3278	  2796	    29	//       probes,         // optional probe map for resolveRoles (default null)
  3279	  2797	    30	//       sessionHint }   // the RECEIVING session's hint, used for ownership adoption
  3280	  2798	    31	//
  3281	  2799	    32	// TOKEN BINDING (THE pin). prepare captures a REVISION FINGERPRINT and returns it
  3282	  2800	    33	//   as an opaque token. It binds every one of these inputs (one rejection test per
  3283	  2801	    34	//   input below — verifier fold F3 completed the set):
  3284	  2802	    35	//     - bundle REVISION: generation + journalSeq (as loadBundle reports them)
  3285	  2803	    36	//     - intake: origin + reason
  3286	  2804	    37	//     - config digest: a hash of the baton.config.json bytes at <root>
  3287	  2805	    38	//     - GIT digest: a hash of the WHOLE opts.gitSnapshot object (PIN — the git
  3288	  2806	    39	//       state is bound by hashing the caller-passed object, so headSha, dirty,
  3289	  2807	    40	//       AND contentDigest are each binding: a HEAD move with an UNCHANGED
  3290	  2808	    41	//       contentDigest still drifts the token, and vice versa)
  3291	  2809	    42	//     - probe snapshot: a hash of opts.probes
  3292	  2810	    43	//     - target platform (opts.platform)
  3293	  2811	    44	//     - receiving session (opts.sessionHint) — plan: the token binds "target
  3294	  2812	    45	//       platform+session"
  3295	  2813	    46	//   prepare mutates NOTHING (it cannot persist a "prepared" record — the token is
  3296	  2814	    47	//   self-contained). commit RE-DERIVES every bound input from current disk state +
  3297	  2815	    48	//   its own opts and compares to the token; ANY drift throws an Error whose message
  3298	  2816	    49	//   names re-prepare (matches /re-?prepare/i) and performs NO mutation (the
  3299	  2817	    50	//   .handoff tree is byte-identical before and after the rejected commit). Because
  3300	  2818	    51	//   a successful commit bumps `generation`, an already-spent token (double-commit)
  3301	  2819	    52	//   and a losing competitor's token (competing receivers) both fail the revision
  3302	  2820	    53	//   check with no extra bookkeeping.
  3303	  2821	    54	//
  3304	  2822	    55	// COMMIT SUCCESS (one atomic transition):
  3305	  2823	    56	//   - Archives the current bundle to history/ via rotateJournal kind 'receive'
  3306	  2824	    57	//     (a history/<stem>.receive.json + .ndjson pair). archivedTo === that stem.
  3307	  2825	    58	//     The archived .receive freeze is the RECEIVED seal (handoff.status
  3308	  2826	    59	//     'received' — asserted on BOTH the sealed and open paths, verifier fold F7:
  3309	  2827	    60	//     archiving the pre-commit sealed bundle unchanged would be wrong); it
  3310	  2828	    61	//     retains the receipt data (the receive_log entry for THIS receive rides in
  3311	  2829	    62	//     the archived freeze). For an OPEN source bundle it is the receive-generated
  3312	  2830	    63	//     DEGRADED seal (handoff.reason from intake, reasonClass classified from the
  3313	  2831	    64	//     reason text against the intake ORIGIN platform via the built-in signature
  3314	  2832	    65	//     table); for a SEALED source the finalize reason/reasonClass are retained.
  3315	  2833	    66	//   - Adopts ownership: origin becomes the RECEIVING side — origin.platform ===
  3316	  2834	    67	//     opts.platform, origin.sessionHint === opts.sessionHint.
  3317	  2835	    68	//   - Opens a fresh writable generation: generation + 1, handoff.status 'open'.
  3318	  2836	    69	//   - Appends a receive_log entry { origin, reason, at }; for the OPEN (degraded)
  3319	  2837	    70	//     path the entry additionally carries degradedSeal: true (PIN: that flag lives
  3320	  2838	    71	//     on the receive_log entry). The sealed path's entry has NO truthy degradedSeal.
  3321	  2839	    72	//   - Returns { adopted: <truthy>, generation: <new gen>, archivedTo: <stem> }.
  3322	  2840	    73	//
  3323	  2841	    74	// STALENESS (prepare warnings, plan §Receive): OPEN source bundle -> a warning
  3324	  2842	    75	//   notes the missing seal; age > 12h (now - finalizedAt), HEAD moved
  3325	  2843	    76	//   (bundle.git.headSha !== opts.gitSnapshot.headSha), and a dirty mismatch each
  3326	  2844	    77	//   add a warning. Asserted as substring matches, not byte-exact strings.
  3327	  2845	    78	//
  3328	  2846	    79	// All txn tests seed: the bundle, baton.config.json at <root>, AND the built-in
  3329	  2847	    80	// signature table at its module-resolved path (loadSignatures reads via io.fs), so
  3330	  2848	    81	// role resolution and reasonClass classification both work over memfs.
  3331	  2849	    82	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3332	  2850	    83	
  3333	  2851	    84	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
  3334	  2852	    85	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');

  3342	  2860	    93	
  3343	  2861	    94	const CONFIG = {
  3344	  2862	    95	  schema: 'baton/config@1',
  3345	  2863	    96	  roles: {

  3353	  2871	   104	  },

  3356	  2874	   107	};
  3357	  2875	   108	const CONFIG_TEXT = JSON.stringify(CONFIG, null, 2);
  3358	  2876	   109	
  3359	  2877	   110	// A git snapshot as core/src/git/snapshot.mjs would return; headSha matches the
  3360	  2878	   111	// bundle's captured git so the non-stale tests raise no HEAD-moved warning.
  3361	  2879	   112	const G1 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-1' };
  3362	  2880	   113	const G2 = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'gd-2-CHANGED' };
  3363	  2881	   114	
  3364	  2882	   115	const snapText = (b) => JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n';
  3365	  2883	   116	
  3366	  2884	   117	function baseBundle(overrides = {}) {
  3367	  2885	   118	  return {
  3368	  2886	   119	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
  3369	  2887	   120	    bundleId: 'b_txn0000000000',
  3370	  2888	   121	    generation: 1,
  3371	  2889	   122	    createdAt: NOW,
  3372	  2890	   123	    updatedAt: NOW,
  3373	  2891	   124	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-A', unstable: false },
  3374	  2892	   125	    task: { goal: 'Wire the receive transaction', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
  3375	  2893	   126	    plan: { steps: [{ id: 's1', title: 'Bind the token', status: 'active', note: null }] },
  3376	  2894	   127	    decisions: [],
  3377	  2895	   128	    files: { touched: [] },
  3378	  2896	   129	    roles: { assignments: {} },
  3379	  2897	   130	    git: { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'd0', summaryTruncated: false },
  3380	  2898	   131	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
  3381	  2899	   132	    journalSeq: 5,
  3382	  2900	   133	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
  3383	  2901	   134	    dedupeRing: [],
  3384	  2902	   135	    ...overrides,
  3385	  2903	   136	  };
  3386	  2904	   137	}
  3387	  2905	   138	
  3388	  2906	   139	function sealedBundle(overrides = {}) {
  3389	  2907	   140	  return baseBundle({
  3390	  2908	   141	    handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: "You've hit your usage limit", reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: 'codex', finalizedAt: NOW, receive_log: [] },
  3391	  2909	   142	    ...overrides,
  3392	  2910	   143	  });
  3393	  2911	   144	}
  3394	  2912	   145	
  3395	  2913	   146	function openBundle(overrides = {}) {
  3396	  2914	   147	  return baseBundle({ handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] }, ...overrides });
  3397	  2915	   148	}
  3398	  2916	   149	
  3399	  2917	   150	function makeTxnIo({ bundle, journal, configText = CONFIG_TEXT, now = NOW, extra = {} } = {}) {
  3400	  2918	   151	  const files = {
  3401	  2919	   152	    [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT,
  3402	  2920	   153	    [`${ROOT}/baton.config.json`]: configText,
  3403	  2921	   154	    [`${ROOT}/.handoff/bundle.json`]: snapText(bundle),
  3404	  2922	   155	    ...extra,
  3405	  2923	   156	  };
  3406	  2924	   157	  if (journal !== undefined) files[`${ROOT}/.handoff/journal.ndjson`] = journal;
  3407	  2925	   158	  return makeIo({ files, now });
  3408	  2926	   159	}
  3409	  2927	   160	
  3410	  2928	   161	// Standard opts for a claude-code -> codex receive.
  3411	  2929	   162	function opts(overrides = {}) {
  3412	  2930	   163	  return { platform: 'codex', origin: 'claude-code', reason: 'switching to codex', gitSnapshot: G1, probes: null, sessionHint: 'codex-sess-1', ...overrides };
  3413	  2931	   164	}
  3414	  2932	   165	
  3415	  2933	   166	const readHistoryFreeze = (io, stem) => JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${bundlePaths(ROOT).historyDir}/${stem}.json`, 'utf8'));
  3416	  2934	   167

  3418	  2936	   169	describe('receive-txn.prepare — sealed bundle', () => {

  3473	  2991	   224	describe('receive-txn.commit — sealed -> received (normal), ownership adoption + new generation', () => {

  3500	  3018	   251	  });
  3501	  3019	   252	
  3502	  3020	   253	  it('(F7) the archived freeze is the RECEIVED seal with receipt data, not the pre-commit sealed bundle', () => {

  3521	  3039	   272	describe('receive-txn.commit — open -> received (degraded seal)', () => {
  3522	  3040	   273	  it('writes a degraded seal: receive_log entry {degradedSeal:true}, reasonClass classified from the reason', () => {
  3523	  3041	   274	    // A usage-limit reason string for the claude-code origin -> classifies usage-limit.
  3524	  3042	   275	    const reason = "You've hit your session limit · resets 3pm";

  3537	  3055	   288

  3541	  3059	   292	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.status, 'received', 'the archived freeze is the received seal');

  3543	  3061	   294	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'reasonClass is classified from the reason text');
  3544	  3062	   295	  });
  3545	  3063	   296	
  3546	  3064	   297	  it('(F10) a LOW-confidence classification is NOT sealed — reasonClass stays null (no laundering)', () => {
  3547	  3065	   298	    // A cursor "quota exceeded" reason matches usage-limit only at LOW
  3548	  3066	   299	    // confidence. Sealing it would make the NEXT prepare trust it as high
  3549	  3067	   300	    // confidence and auto-avoid the origin. The degraded seal must leave
  3550	  3068	   301	    // reasonClass null so the next receive re-classifies at its true confidence.
  3551	  3069	   302	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: openBundle(), journal: '' });

  3557	  3075	   308	    assert.equal(seal.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'a low-confidence class is not laundered into the seal');
  3558	  3076	   309

  3560	  3078	   311	    const { bundle } = loadBundle(ROOT, io);
  3561	  3079	   312	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'the new open generation inherits no low-confidence class');
  3562	  3080	   313	  });

  3572	  3090	   323	  it('(iter-4 I3) the fresh open generation carries NO prior reason/class/finalizedAt/toPlatformHint', () => {
  3573	  3091	   324	    // Even a HIGH-confidence sealed usage-limit receive must not leave its class
  3574	  3092	   325	    // on the new writable generation: the next prepare treats a sealed
  3575	  3093	   326	    // reasonClass as high-confidence and would auto-avoid the freshly adopted
  3576	  3094	   327	    // origin. Only receive_log (the audit chain) carries forward.
  3577	  3095	   328	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3578	  3096	   329	    const o = opts();
  3579	  3097	   330	    const { token } = prepare(ROOT, o, io);
  3580	  3098	   331	    const res = commit(ROOT, token, o, io);

  3584	  3102	   335	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'a fresh writable generation');
  3585	  3103	   336	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reason, null, 'no carried reason');
  3586	  3104	   337	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, null, 'no carried class (would launder as high-confidence next hop)');
  3587	  3105	   338	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.finalizedAt, null, 'no carried finalizedAt (would poison the staleness reference)');
  3588	  3106	   339	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.toPlatformHint, null, 'no carried toPlatformHint');
  3589	  3107	   340	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(bundle.handoff.receive_log) && bundle.handoff.receive_log.length >= 1, 'the receive_log audit chain is kept');
  3590	  3108	   341	  });
  3591	  3109	   342	
  3592	  3110	   343	  it('(iter-4 I3) a receive off the adopted generation does NOT auto-avoid the healthy origin', () => {
  3593	  3111	   344	    // End-to-end of the laundering fix: after a sealed usage-limit receive to
  3594	  3112	   345	    // claude-code, a NEXT receive (open generation, no seal) must re-classify at
  3595	  3113	   346	    // true confidence, not trust a carried class → claude-code stays selectable.
  3596	  3114	   347	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3597	  3115	   348	    const o1 = opts(); // claude-code -> codex per the helper default
  3598	  3116	   349	    commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, o1, io).token, o1, io);
  3599	  3117	   350	    // Now generation 2 is open, owned by codex. Receive it onward with a benign
  3600	  3118	   351	    // (non-limit) reason; the carried class must not drive avoidance.
  3601	  3119	   352	    const o2 = opts({ platform: 'claude-code', origin: 'codex', reason: 'switching back', sessionHint: 'cc-2' });
  3602	  3120	   353	    const { assignments } = prepare(ROOT, o2, io);
  3603	  3121	   354	    const avoidedCodex = Object.values(assignments).every((/** @type {any} */ a) =>
  3604	  3122	   355	      (a.skipped ?? []).some((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === 'codex' && s.why === 'avoided'),
  3605	  3123	   356	    );
  3606	  3124	   357	    assert.equal(avoidedCodex, false, 'no carried class silently avoids the adopted origin');
  3607	  3125	   358	  });
  3608	  3126	   359	});

  3610	  3128	   361	// ===========================================================================
  3611	  3129	   362	describe('receive-txn — chained failover A -> B -> C (checkpoints fired after each receive, F8)', () => {
  3612	  3130	   363	  // Plan §Concurrency: "chained A→B→C failover is contract-tested with checkpoints
  3613	  3131	   364	  // fired immediately after each receive". At this altitude a checkpoint is a
  3614	  3132	   365	  // journal append from the ADOPTED session; first-party = the event's sessionHint
  3615	  3133	   366	  // matches the live bundle's adopted origin.sessionHint and the event folds in.
  3616	  3134	   367	  it('receives twice with a first-party checkpoint after each; generations 1->2->3, ownership claude-code->codex->cursor', () => {
  3617	  3135	   368	    const io = makeTxnIo({ bundle: sealedBundle(), journal: '' });
  3618	  3136	   369	
  3619	  3137	   370	    // A (claude-code) -> B (codex).
  3620	  3138	   371	    const oAB = opts({ platform: 'codex', origin: 'claude-code', reason: 'A hit limit', sessionHint: 'codex-s' });
  3621	  3139	   372	    const rAB = commit(ROOT, prepare(ROOT, oAB, io).token, oAB, io);

  3623	  3141	   374	
  3624	  3142	   375	    // Checkpoint immediately after the receive, from the adopted session.
  3625	  3143	   376	    appendJournal(ROOT, { type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'ck-B', source: 'stop', ts: NOW, sessionHint: 'codex-s', payload: { summary: 'B-first-party' } }, io);

  3633	  3151	   384	    // checkpoint does NOT block a subsequent receive.

  3711	  3229	exec
  3712	  3230	/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba tests/unit/store.test.mjs | sed -n '1,280p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
  3713	  3231	 succeeded in 0ms:
  3714	  3232	     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';

  3722	  3240	     9	  rotateJournal,

  3724	  3242	    11	import { applyEvent } from '../../core/src/bundle/merge.mjs';
  3725	  3243	    12	import { readAllTolerant } from '../../core/src/util/jsonl.mjs';
  3726	  3244	    13	
  3727	  3245	    14	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3728	  3246	    15	// Contract choices for bundle/store.mjs (docs/design/core.md §Module APIs,
  3729	  3247	    16	// §store test list; plan §Bundle, §Concurrency, §"Journal rotation & crash
  3730	  3248	    17	// recovery"). Readings pinned where the design left room:
  3731	  3249	    18	//
  3732	  3250	    19	//   1. PATHS — everything is rooted at `<root>/.handoff/`. `.bak` is the
  3733	  3251	    20	//      snapshot path + ".bak" (i.e. bundle.json.bak), matching fsx.backupThenWrite
  3734	  3252	    21	//      and fsx documentation. Exact set asserted in the bundlePaths test.
  3735	  3253	    22	//   2. loadBundle NULL — returns {bundle:null, warnings:[]} when the .handoff
  3736	  3254	    23	//      dir is absent OR neither a snapshot nor a journal exists. Warnings is
  3737	  3255	    24	//      ALWAYS an array of human-readable STRINGS (the API declares string[]);
  3738	  3256	    25	//      jsonl's structured {kind,line} warnings are surfaced as formatted strings,
  3739	  3257	    26	//      not raw objects.
  3740	  3258	    27	//   3. REPLAY — with a valid snapshot present, loadBundle applies exactly the
  3741	  3259	    28	//      journal entries whose seq > bundle.journalSeq, in journal (file) order,
  3742	  3260	    29	//      via merge.applyEvent. This must equal a pure left-fold of those same
  3743	  3261	    30	//      entries over the snapshot — the load path adds no hidden behavior. The
  3744	  3262	    31	//      seq>journalSeq FILTER is load-owned and independent of dedupe: the
  3745	  3263	    32	//      replay-boundary test drives a snapshot with an EMPTY dedupeRing so an
  3746	  3264	    33	//      implementation that wrongly folds the WHOLE journal cannot hide behind the
  3747	  3265	    34	//      ring — the pre-boundary events would visibly reappear in decisions.
  3748	  3266	    35	//   4. RECOVERY LADDER — snapshot that fails to parse OR fails validateBundle →
  3749	  3267	    36	//      try bundle.json.bak (parse+validate); if that is good, replay over IT
  3750	  3268	    37	//      (seq > bak.journalSeq). If BOTH snapshot and .bak are unusable → full
  3751	  3269	    38	//      rebuild by seeding an empty bundle and replaying the WHOLE journal. Every
  3752	  3270	    39	//      recovery emits at least one warning string.
  3753	  3271	    40	//   5. TORN TAIL — a torn final journal line is tolerated (jsonl drops it); the
  3754	  3272	    41	//      torn-tail warning is surfaced. Leftover *.tmp.* siblings are ignored by
  3755	  3273	    42	//      load (safeReadJson reads the real file).
  3756	  3274	    43	//   6. ROTATION FILE FORMAT — the history stem is
  3757	  3275	    44	//         `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`  where  tsForFile(iso)=iso.replace(/[:.]/g,'-')
  3758	  3276	    45	//      producing `history/<stem>.json` (frozen snapshot copy) and
  3759	  3277	    46	//      `history/<stem>.ndjson` (rotated journal). rotateJournal RETURNS the stem.
  3760	  3278	    47	//      The transform is pinned HERE so the manually-constructed crash states in
  3761	  3279	    48	//      the roll-forward/back tests name files exactly where loadBundle looks.
  3762	  3280	    49	//   7. ROTATION ORDER (crash-safe, plan §rotation): write
  3763	  3281	    50	//      `.handoff/rotation.marker.json` {kind, startedAt, seq} → freeze snapshot
  3764	  3282	    51	//      to history/<stem>.json → rename journal to history/<stem>.ndjson → start a
  3765	  3283	    52	//      fresh empty journal → clear the marker.
  3766	  3284	    53	//   8. MARKER RECONCILIATION — on load, a leftover marker rolls FORWARD when the
  3767	  3285	    54	//      freeze (history/<stem>.json, stem recomputed from marker.startedAt+kind)
  3768	  3286	    55	//      landed, else rolls BACK (clear marker, leave the live journal untouched).
  3769	  3287	    56	//      Both cases warn. Roll-forward completes from WHICHEVER step the crash
  3770	  3288	    57	//      interrupted: if the live journal has not yet been renamed to
  3771	  3289	    58	//      history/<stem>.ndjson, load performs that rename (losing no entries) and
  3772	  3290	    59	//      then seeds a fresh empty live journal; the near-complete state (freeze +
  3773	  3291	    60	//      rotated journal + empty live journal already present) is handled too.
  3774	  3292	    61	//   9. RETENTION — after a rotation, only the newest 10 rotations PER KIND are
  3775	  3293	    62	//      kept in history/ (both the .json and .ndjson of pruned rotations removed).
  3776	  3294	    63	//  10. writeSnapshot backs up the previous good snapshot to .bak, writes the new
  3777	  3295	    64	//      snapshot atomically (tmp+rename, via fsx), and renders HANDOFF.md
  3778	  3296	    65	//      alongside by delegating to render.mjs. This file proves the delegation
  3779	  3297	    66	//      via render's signature (the pinned footer + the goal) WITHOUT importing
  3780	  3298	    67	//      render — render.test owns the exact format.
  3781	  3299	    68	//  11. appendJournal runs UNDER THE REPO LOCK (see 12), ensures `.handoff/`
  3782	  3300	    69	//      exists, appends the entry via jsonl, and returns the entry's seq. SEQ
  3783	  3301	    70	//      ALLOCATION: an entry that already carries a numeric `seq` keeps it
  3784	  3302	    71	//      (adapter-normalized events arrive pre-numbered). An entry that LACKS a seq
  3785	  3303	    72	//      is assigned the next one under the lock — max(snapshot.journalSeq, highest
  3786	  3304	    73	//      seq already in the journal) + 1, monotonic across calls — and that
  3787	  3305	    74	//      allocated seq is both stamped onto the appended entry AND returned.
  3788	  3306	    75	//  12. REPO LOCK — writeSnapshot, appendJournal, and rotateJournal are MUTATIONS
  3789	  3307	    76	//      and run under bundle/lock.mjs's withLock (acquire → mutate → release).
  3790	  3308	    77	//      With a LIVE same-host foreign owner already holding `.handoff/lock/`, each
  3791	  3309	    78	//      throws LockHeldError (surfaced verbatim from the lock layer) and leaves
  3792	  3310	    79	//      every file byte-for-byte unchanged — a refused mutation is a no-op.
  3793	  3311	    80	//      loadBundle is a READ/recovery path and is NOT lock-gated (marker
  3794	  3312	    81	//      reconciliation is part of load).
  3795	  3313	    82	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3796	  3314	    83	
  3797	  3315	    84	const T0 = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';

  4313	  3831	   543	describe('store.rotateJournal', () => {
  4314	  3832	   544	  it('happy path: freezes the snapshot + journal into history, empties the live journal, clears the marker', () => {
  4315	  3833	   545	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 5 });

  4331	  3849	   561

  4339	  3857	   569	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(paths.dir + '/rotation.marker.json'), false, 'the marker is cleared on success');
  4340	  3858	   570	  });
  4341	  3859	   571	
  4342	  3860	   572	  it('retention: keeps only the newest 10 rotations PER KIND', () => {
  4343	  3861	   573	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 0 });
  4344	  3862	   574	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap) } });
  4345	  3863	   575	    const paths = bundlePaths('/repo');
  4346	  3864	   576	
  4347	  3865	   577	    // Two takeover rotations — these must survive finalize pruning (per-kind).
  4348	  3866	   578	    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
  4349	  3867	   579	      io.setNow(`2026-07-11T10:0${i}:00.000Z`);
  4350	  3868	   580	      appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 200 + i, dedupeKey: `tk${i}` }), io);
  4351	  3869	   581	      rotateJournal('/repo', 'takeover', io);
  4352	  3870	   582	    }
  4353	  3871	   583	
  4354	  3872	   584	    // Eleven finalize rotations — the oldest must be pruned to 10.
  4355	  3873	   585	    const finalizeStems = [];
  4356	  3874	   586	    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i += 1) {
  4357	  3875	   587	      const mm = String(i).padStart(2, '0');

  4359	  3877	   589	      appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 1 + i, dedupeKey: `fk${i}` }), io);
  4360	  3878	   590	      finalizeStems.push(rotateJournal('/repo', 'finalize', io));
  4361	  3879	   591	    }
  4362	  3880	   592	
  4363	  3881	   593	    const names = io.fs.readdirSync(paths.historyDir);
  4364	  3882	   594	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.finalize\.json$/.test(n)).length, 10, 'exactly 10 finalize freezes retained');
  4365	  3883	   595	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.finalize\.ndjson$/.test(n)).length, 10, 'exactly 10 finalize journals retained');
  4366	  3884	   596	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.takeover\.json$/.test(n)).length, 2, 'takeover rotations pruned independently');
  4367	  3885	   597	
  4368	  3886	   598	    assert.ok(!names.includes(`${finalizeStems[0]}.json`), 'the oldest finalize freeze was pruned');
  4369	  3887	   599	    assert.ok(names.includes(`${finalizeStems[10]}.json`), 'the newest finalize freeze was kept');
  4370	  3888	   600	  });
  4371	  3889	   601	});

  4373	  3891	   603	describe('store — mutations run under the repo lock', () => {
  4374	  3892	   604	  // With a LIVE same-host foreign owner holding `.handoff/lock/`, every store
  4375	  3893	   605	  // MUTATION must be refused by the lock layer (LockHeldError) and leave the
  4376	  3894	   606	  // whole `.handoff/` tree byte-for-byte unchanged. This is what proves the
  4377	  3895	   607	  // mutations actually acquire the lock rather than writing unlocked.
  4378	  3896	   608	  const withLiveForeignLock = (files) =>
  4379	  3897	   609	    makeIo({
  4380	  3898	   610	      files: { [LOCK_OWNER]: JSON.stringify(liveForeignOwner), ...files },
  4381	  3899	   611	      processAlive: (pid) => pid === 777 || pid === 4242, // pid 777 (the owner) is alive
  4382	  3900	   612	    });
  4383	  3901	   613	  const isLockHeld = (e) => e && e.name === 'LockHeldError';
  4384	  3902	   614	
  4385	  3903	   615	  it('writeSnapshot throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  4386	  3904	   616	    const prev = mkBundle({ task: { goal: 'PREV goal', constraints: [], acceptance: [] } });
  4387	  3905	   617	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({ '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(prev) });
  4388	  3906	   618	    const before = io.files();
  4389	  3907	   619	
  4390	  3908	   620	    assert.throws(
  4391	  3909	   621	      () => writeSnapshot('/repo', mkBundle({ generation: 2, task: { goal: 'NEW goal', constraints: [], acceptance: [] } }), io),
  4392	  3910	   622	      isLockHeld,
  4393	  3911	   623	    );
  4394	  3912	   624	
  4395	  3913	   625	    const after = io.files();
  4396	  3914	   626	    assert.deepEqual(after, before, 'no file may change when a live foreign owner holds the lock');
  4397	  3915	   627	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json'], snapText(prev), 'the existing snapshot is untouched');
  4398	  3916	   628	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json.bak'], undefined, 'no .bak is written under a refused lock');
  4399	  3917	   629	    assert.equal(after['/repo/.handoff/HANDOFF.md'], undefined, 'HANDOFF.md is not rendered under a refused lock');
  4400	  3918	   630	    assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(after[LOCK_OWNER]), liveForeignOwner, 'the foreign owner.json is never modified');
  4401	  3919	   631	  });
  4402	  3920	   632	
  4403	  3921	   633	  it('appendJournal throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  4404	  3922	   634	    const existing = [ev({ seq: 6, dedupeKey: 'e6' })];
  4405	  3923	   635	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({ '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': journalText(existing) });
  4406	  3924	   636	    const before = io.files();
  4407	  3925	   637	
  4408	  3926	   638	    assert.throws(() => appendJournal('/repo', ev({ seq: 7, dedupeKey: 'e7' }), io), isLockHeld);
  4409	  3927	   639	    assert.throws(() => appendJournal('/repo', evNoSeq({ dedupeKey: 'e8' }), io), isLockHeld);
  4410	  3928	   640	
  4411	  3929	   641	    assert.deepEqual(io.files(), before, 'the journal is not appended (and no seq allocated) under a refused lock');
  4412	  3930	   642	    assert.equal(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson'], journalText(existing), 'the journal bytes are unchanged');
  4413	  3931	   643	  });
  4414	  3932	   644	
  4415	  3933	   645	  it('rotateJournal throws LockHeldError under a live foreign lock and changes nothing', () => {
  4416	  3934	   646	    const snap = mkBundle({ journalSeq: 5 });
  4417	  3935	   647	    const entries = [ev({ seq: 4, dedupeKey: 'a' }), ev({ seq: 5, dedupeKey: 'b' })];
  4418	  3936	   648	    const io = withLiveForeignLock({
  4419	  3937	   649	      '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': snapText(snap),
  4420	  3938	   650	      '/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson': journalText(entries),
  4421	  3939	   651	    });
  4422	  3940	   652	    const before = io.files();
  4423	  3941	   653

  4425	  3943	   655	
  4426	  3944	   656	    const after = io.files();
  4427	  3945	   657	    assert.deepEqual(after, before, 'nothing rotates when a live foreign owner holds the lock');

  4429	  3947	   659	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/rotation.marker.json'), false, 'no rotation marker is written under a refused lock');
  4430	  3948	   660	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/history'), false, 'no history freeze is created under a refused lock');

  4525	  4043	   292	    assert.equal(b.decisions.length, 501);

  4529	  4047	   296	
  4530	  4048	   297	    // Re-applying the most-recent event (still in ring) is a no-op.
  4531	  4049	   298	    const afterRecent = applyEvent(b, events[500]);
  4532	  4050	   299	    assert.equal(afterRecent.decisions.length, 501, 'a still-ringed event must not re-apply');
  4533	  4051	   300

  5418	     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
  5419	     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
  5420	     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
  5421	     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
  5422	     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
  5423	     6	//
  5424	     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
  5425	     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
  5426	     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
  5427	    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
  5428	    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
  5429	    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
  5430	    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
  5431	    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
  5432	    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
  5433	    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
  5434	    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
  5435	    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
  5436	    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
  5437	    20	
  5438	    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
  5439	    22	
  5440	    23	export function makeIo({
  5441	    24	  files = {},
  5442	    25	  env = {},
  5443	    26	  stdin = '',
  5444	    27	  execResults = {},
  5445	    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
  5446	    29	  host = 'host-A',
  5447	    30	  pid = 4242,
  5448	    31	  startTime = 111000,
  5449	    32	  processAlive = null,
  5450	    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
  5451	    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
  5452	    35	} = {}) {
  5453	    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
  5454	    37	  const outChunks = [];
  5455	    38	  const errChunks = [];
  5456	    39	  let currentNow = now;
  5457	    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
  5458	    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
  5459	    42	
  5460	    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  5461	    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
  5462	    45	
  5463	    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
  5464	    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
  5465	    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  5466	    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
  5467	    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
  5468	    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
  5469	    52	        return reject(e);
  5470	    53	      }
  5471	    54	      const r = execResults[key];
  5472	    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
  5473	    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
  5474	    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
  5475	    58	      if (code !== 0) {
  5476	    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
  5477	    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
  5478	    61	        e.code = code;
  5479	    62	        e.stdout = out;
  5480	    63	        e.stderr = err;
  5481	    64	        return reject(e);
  5482	    65	      }
  5483	    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
  5484	    67	    });
  5485	    68	  };
  5486	    69	
  5487	    70	  return {
  5488	    71	    cwd: '/repo',
  5489	    72	    env,
  5490	    73	    stdin,
  5491	    74	    stdout,
  5492	    75	    stderr,
  5493	    76	    fs,
  5494	    77	    execFile,
  5495	    78	    now: () => currentNow,
  5496	    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
  5497	    80	    execPath,
  5498	    81	    host,
  5499	    82	    pid,
  5500	    83	    startTime,
  5501	    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
  5502	    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
  5503	    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
  5504	    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
  5505	    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
  5506	    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
  5507	    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
  5508	    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
  5509	    92	  };
  5510	    93	}

  6018	   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
  6019	   140	
  6020	   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
  6021	   142	
  6022	   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
  6023	   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
  6024	   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
  6025	   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
  6026	   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
  6027	   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
  6028	   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
  6029	   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
  6030	   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
  6031	   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
  6032	   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
  6033	   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
  6034	   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
  6035	   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
  6036	   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
  6037	   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
  6038	   159	
  6039	   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
  6040	   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
  6041	   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
  6042	   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
  6043	   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
  6044	   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
  6045	   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
  6046	   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
  6047	   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
  6048	   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
  6049	   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
  6050	   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
  6051	   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
  6052	   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
  6053	   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the
  6054

  6057	    70

  6066	    79	
  6067	    80	One authoritative root: the MAIN repo root where `loop.json` lives. The
  6068	    81	supervisor is the SOLE writer of `.handoff/loop/` and of the main root's
  6069	    82	handoff bundle; every supervisor-driven baton invocation passes explicit
  6070	    83	`--root <mainRoot>` and a supervisor-owned stable session hint
  6071	    84	(`--session loop-<runId>` — a NEW flag added to `checkpoint`; `receive`
  6072	    85	already has it), so the bundle has exactly one stable owner.
  6073	    86	
  6074	    87	**Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3,
  6075	    88	finding 1)** — children never touch OR read any bundle, by construction: the
  6076	    89	supervisor sets `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1` in every child's environment, and
  6077	    90	EVERY baton hook command — the hook entrypoints, `baton checkpoint`, and
  6078	    91	`baton session-start` (which would otherwise read the bundle and inject
  6079	    92	pending-handoff resume context into the child) — quietly no-ops (exit 0,

  6186	     9

  6190	    13

  6192	    15	
  6193	    16	/**
  6194	    17	 * The scaffold `baton loop init` writes. Phase ids are progress labels;
  6195	    18	 * every role is a matrix role ID resolved via baton.config.json — never a

  6199	    22	 */

  6202	    25	    schema: LOOP_SCHEMA,

  6210	    33	      // failing smoke assertion → verifier approval → minimal implementation.

  6219	    42	      { id: 'gate-2-b', role: 'final-reviewer-b' },
  6220	    43	    ],
  6221	    44	    budgets: { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS },
  6222	    45	  };
  6223	    46	}
  6224	    47	
  6225	    48	/**
  6226	    49	 * Validate a loop spec against the role matrix. Reports EVERY problem at
  6227	    50	 * once (each error names its offender) so a spec author fixes one round, not
  6228	    51	 * one field per run. Valid specs are returned normalized: caller-provided
  6229	    52	 * fields are preserved verbatim; only ABSENT constraints/budgets/smoke are
  6230	    53	 * filled with defaults.
  6231	    54	 * @param {any} spec
  6232	    55	 * @param {any} config the parsed baton.config.json (roles are checked against it)
  6233	    56	 * @returns {{ok: true, spec: any} | {ok: false, errors: string[]}}
  6234	    57	 */
  6235	    58	export function validateLoopSpec(spec, config) {
  6236	    59	  /** @type {string[]} */
  6237	    60	  const errors = [];
  6238	    61	  const s = spec ?? {};
  6239	    62	
  6240	    63	  if (s.schema !== LOOP_SCHEMA) errors.push(`schema must be '${LOOP_SCHEMA}' (got ${JSON.stringify(s.schema)})`);
  6241	    64	  if (typeof s.goal !== 'string' || s.goal.length === 0) errors.push('goal must be a non-empty string');
  6242	    65	
  6243	    66	  let constraints = s.constraints;
  6244	    67	  if (constraints === undefined) constraints = [];
  6245	    68	  if (!Array.isArray(constraints)) {
  6246	    69	    errors.push('constraints must be an array of strings');
  6247	    70	  } else {
  6248	    71	    constraints.forEach((c, i) => {
  6249	    72	      if (typeof c !== 'string') errors.push(`constraints[${i}] must be a string (got ${JSON.stringify(c)})`);
  6250	    73	    });
  6251	    74	  }
  6252	    75	
  6253	    76	  const roles = config?.roles ?? {};
  6254	    77	  if (!Array.isArray(s.phases) || s.phases.length === 0) {
  6255	    78	    errors.push('phases must be a non-empty array of {id, role}');
  6256	    79	  } else {
  6257	    80	    s.phases.forEach((/** @type {any} */ p, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
  6258	    81	      if (typeof p?.id !== 'string' || p.id.length === 0) errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing an id (a non-empty progress label)`);
  6259	    82	      if (typeof p?.role !== 'string' || p.role.length === 0) {
  6260	    83	        errors.push(`phases[${i}] is missing a role (a baton.config.json role ID)`);
  6261	    84	      } else if (!(p.role in roles)) {
  6262	    85	        errors.push(`phases[${i}]: unknown role '${p.role}' — add it to baton.config.json roles or fix the reference`);
  6263	    86	      }
  6264	    87	    });
  6265	    88	  }
  6266	    89	
  6267	    90	  let budgets = s.budgets;
  6268	    91	  if (budgets === undefined) {
  6269	    92	    budgets = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGETS };
  6270	    93	  } else if (typeof budgets !== 'object' || budgets === null) {
  6271	    94	    errors.push('budgets must be an object');
  6272	    95	  } else {
  6273	    96	    const cap = budgets.iterationCap;
  6274	    97	    if (typeof cap !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(cap) || cap < 1 || cap > ITERATION_CAP_MAX) {
  6275	    98	      errors.push(`budgets.iterationCap must be a number between 1 and ${ITERATION_CAP_MAX} (the 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant; got ${JSON.stringify(cap)})`);
  6276	    99	    }
  6277	   100	    for (const field of ['perRoleTimeoutMin', 'maxChildrenPerPhase']) {
  6278	   101	      const v = budgets[field];
  6279	   102	      if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) {
  6280	   103	        errors.push(`budgets.${field} must be a positive number (got ${JSON.stringify(v)})`);
  6281	   104	      }
  6282	   105	    }
  6283	   106	  }
  6284	   107	
  6285	   108	  let smoke = s.smoke;
  6286	   109	  if (smoke === undefined) {
  6287	   110	    smoke = { cmd: null, expect: null };
  6288	   111	  } else if (typeof smoke !== 'object' || smoke === null) {
  6289	   112	    errors.push('smoke must be an object ({cmd, expect}, each a string or null)');
  6290	   113	  } else {
  6291	   114	    if (!(smoke.cmd === null || typeof smoke.cmd === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.cmd must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.cmd)})`);
  6292	   115	    if (!(smoke.expect === null || typeof smoke.expect === 'string')) errors.push(`smoke.expect must be a string or null (got ${JSON.stringify(smoke.expect)})`);
  6293	   116	  }
  6294	   117	
  6295	   118	  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
  6296	   119	  return { ok: true, spec: { ...s, constraints, budgets, smoke } };
  6297	   120	}

  6526	     1	// In-memory fs fake for unit tests. Synchronous subset only.
  6527	     2	// Atomicity contract: renameSync moves content in a single step, so a target
  6528	     3	// path is never observed carrying partial content. Every mutation appends a
  6529	     4	// full snapshot to `fs.__history`, letting tests enumerate intermediate states
  6530	     5	// and prove the tmp+rename discipline (target's first appearance is a rename).
  6531	     6	
  6532	     7	function fsError(code, syscall, path) {
  6533	     8	  const e = new Error(`${code}: ${syscall} '${path}'`);
  6534	     9	  e.code = code;
  6535	    10	  e.syscall = syscall;
  6536	    11	  e.path = path;
  6537	    12	  return e;
  6538	    13	}

  6540	    15	function normalize(p) {
  6541	    16	  const str = String(p);
  6542	    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
  6543	    18	  const out = [];
  6544	    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
  6545	    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
  6546	    21	    if (part === '..') {
  6547	    22	      out.pop();
  6548	    23	      continue;
  6549	    24	    }
  6550	    25	    out.push(part);
  6551	    26	  }
  6552	    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
  6553	    28	}

  6555	    30	function dirname(np) {
  6556	    31	  const i = np.lastIndexOf('/');
  6557	    32	  if (i <= 0) return '/';
  6558	    33	  return np.slice(0, i);
  6559	    34	}

  6561	    36	function basename(np, parent) {
  6562	    37	  return parent === '/' ? np.slice(1) : np.slice(parent.length + 1);
  6563	    38	}

  6565	    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
  6566	    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
  6567	    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
  6568	    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
  6569	    44	  const history = [];
  6570	    45	  let clock = 0;
  6571	    46	
  6572	    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
  6573	    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
  6574	    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
  6575	    50	
  6576	    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
  6577	    52	    let cur = '';
  6578	    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
  6579	    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
  6580	    55	      dirs.add(cur);
  6581	    56	    }
  6582	    57	  };
  6583	    58	
  6584	    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
  6585	    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
  6586	    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
  6587	    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
  6588	    63	  };
  6589	    64	
  6590	    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
  6591	    66	    const np = normalize(p);
  6592	    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
  6593	    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
  6594	    69	    touch(np);
  6595	    70	  }
  6596	    71	
  6597	    72	  const fs = {
  6598	    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
  6599	    74	      const np = normalize(p);
  6600	    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
  6601	    76	      return store.get(np);
  6602	    77	    },
  6603	    78	
  6604	    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
  6605	    80	      const np = normalize(p);
  6606	    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  6607	    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
  6608	    83	      touch(np);
  6609	    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
  6610	    85	    },
  6611	    86	
  6612	    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
  6613	    88	      const np = normalize(p);
  6614	    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
  6615	    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
  6616	    91	      touch(np);
  6617	    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
  6618	    93	    },
  6619	    94	
  6620	    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
  6621	    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
  6622	    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
  6623	    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
  6624	    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  6625	   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
  6626	   101	        store.delete(nf);
  6627	   102	        store.set(nt, content);
  6628	   103	        touch(nt);
  6629	   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  6630	   105	        return;
  6631	   106	      }
  6632	   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
  6633	   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
  6634	   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
  6635	   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
  6636	   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
  6637	   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
  6638	   113	        dirs.add(nt);
  6639	   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
  6640	   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
  6641	   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
  6642	   117	            store.delete(k);
  6643	   118	          }
  6644	   119	        }
  6645	   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
  6646	   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
  6647	   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
  6648	   123	            dirs.delete(d);
  6649	   124	          }
  6650	   125	        }
  6651	   126	        touch(nt);
  6652	   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
  6653	   128	        return;
  6654	   129	      }
  6655	   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
  6656	   131	    },
  6657	   132	
  6658	   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
  6659	   134	      const np = normalize(p);
  6660	   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
  6661	   136	        mkdirp(np);
  6662	   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  6663	   138	        return undefined;
  6664	   139	      }
  6665	   140	      ensureParent(np, 'mkdir');
  6666	   141	      if (dirs.has(np) || store.has(np)) throw fsError('EEXIST', 'mkdir', np);
  6667	   142	      dirs.add(np);
  6668	   143	      record('mkdirSync', [np]);
  6669	   144	      return undefined;
  6670	   145	    },
  6671	   146	
  6672	   147	    existsSync(p) {
  6673	   148	      const np = normalize(p);
  6674	   149	      return store.has(np) || dirs.has(np);
  6675	   150	    },
  6676	   151	
  6677	   152	    readdirSync(p) {
  6678	   153	      const np = normalize(p);
  6679	   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {

  6682	   157	      }
  6683	   158	      const names = new Set();
  6684	   159	      const collect = (k) => {
  6685	   160	        if (k !== np && dirname(k) === np) names.add(basename(k, np));
  6686	   161	      };
  6687	   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
  6688	   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
  6689	   164	      return [...names];
  6690	   165	    },
  6691	   166	
  6692	   167	    unlinkSync(p) {

  6694	   169	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'unlink', np);
  6695	   170	      store.delete(np);
  6696	   171	      record('unlinkSync', [np]);
  6697	   172	    },
  6698	   173	
  6699	   174	    rmSync(p, opts = {}) {
  6700	   175	      const np = normalize(p);
  6701	   176	      const isFile = store.has(np);
  6702	   177	      const isDir = dirs.has(np);
  6703	   178	      if (!isFile && !isDir) {
  6704	   179	        if (opts.force) return;
  6705	   180	        throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rm', np);
  6706	   181	      }
  6707	   182	      if (isFile) store.delete(np);
  6708	   183	      if (isDir) {
  6709	   184	        const pre = np + '/';
  6710	   185	        if (!opts.recursive) {
  6711	   186	          const hasChildren = [...store.keys(), ...dirs].some((k) => k !== np && k.startsWith(pre));

  6713	   188	          dirs.delete(np);

  6716	   191	          for (const d of [...dirs]) if (d === np || d.startsWith(pre)) dirs.delete(d);
  6717	   192	        }
  6718	   193	      }
  6719	   194	      record('rmSync', [np]);
  6720	   195	    },

  6864	   229	/**
  6865	   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
  6866	   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
  6867	   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
  6868	   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  6869	   234	 */
  6870	   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
  6871	   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
  6872	   237	}
  6873	   238	
  6874	   239	/**
  6875	   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
  6876	   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
  6877	   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
  6878	   243	 */
  6879	   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
  6880	   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  6881	   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  6882	   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  6883	   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  6884	   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
  6885	   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
  6886	   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  6887	   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  6888	   253	
  6889	   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
  6890	   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
  6891	   256	
  6892	   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
  6893	   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
  6894	   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
  6895	   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  6896	   261	    } else {
  6897	   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
  6898	   263	    }
  6899	   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  6900	   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
  6901	   266	    } else {
  6902	   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
  6903	   268	    }
  6904	   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  6905	   270	
  6906	   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
  6907	   272	    const freezes = io.fs
  6908	   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
  6909	   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
  6910	   275	      .sort();
  6911	   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
  6912	   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
  6913	   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
  6914	   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
  6915	   280	        try {
  6916	   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
  6917	   282	        } catch {
  6918	   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
  6919	   284	        }
  6920	   285	      }
  6921	   286	    }
  6922	   287	
  6923	   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
  6924	   289	    return stem;
  6925	   290	  });
  6926	   291	}

  6929	     1	import { atomicWriteText, safeReadJson, ensureDir, atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
  6930	     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
  6931	     3	import { checkHandoffTree, assertHandoffTreeSafe } from '../util/jail.mjs';
  6932	     4	import { emptyBundle, validateBundle } from './schema.mjs';

  6934	     6	import { compact } from './compact.mjs';

  6938	    10	const RETAIN_PER_KIND = 10;

  6940	    12	/** @param {string} root */
  6941	    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {
  6942	    14	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
  6943	    15	  return {
  6944	    16	    dir,
  6945	    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,
  6946	    18	    bak: `${dir}/bundle.json.bak`,
  6947	    19	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
  6948	    20	    handoffMd: `${dir}/HANDOFF.md`,
  6949	    21	    historyDir: `${dir}/history`,
  6950	    22	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
  6951	    23	    logDir: `${dir}/log`,
  6952	    24	  };
  6953	    25	}
  6954	    26	
  6955	    27	/** @param {string} iso */
  6956	    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
  6957	    29	
  6958	    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
  6959	    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
  6960	    32	
  6961	    33	/**
  6962	    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
  6963	    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
  6964	    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
  6965	    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
  6966	    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
  6967	    39	 */
  6968	    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
  6969	    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
  6970	    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
  6971	    43	  try {
  6972	    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
  6973	    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
  6974	    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
  6975	    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
  6976	    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  6977	    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
  6978	    50	        return;
  6979	    51	      }
  6980	    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
  6981	    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
  6982	    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
  6983	    55	
  6984	    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
  6985	    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
  6986	    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
  6987	    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
  6988	    60	        }
  6989	    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  6990	    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
  6991	    63	        }
  6992	    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  6993	    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
  6994	    66	      } else {
  6995	    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
  6996	    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
  6997	    69	      }
  6998	    70	    });
  6999	    71	  } catch (err) {
  7000	    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
  7001	    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
  7002	    74	      return;
  7003	    75	    }
  7004	    76	    throw err;
  7005	    77	  }
  7006	    78	}
  7007	    79	
  7008	    80	/**
  7009	    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
  7010	    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
  7011	    83	 */
  7012	    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
  7013	    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
  7014	    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
  7015	    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  7016	    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
  7017	    89	    warnings.push(
  7018	    90	      snap.ok
  7019	    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
  7020	    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
  7021	    93	    );
  7022	    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
  7023	    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
  7024	    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
  7025	    97	      return bak.value;
  7026	    98	    }
  7027	    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
  7028	   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  7029	   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
  7030	   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  7031	   103	    }
  7032	   104	    return null;
  7033	   105	  }
  7034	   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  7035	   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
  7036	   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
  7037	   109	  }
  7038	   110	  return null;
  7039	   111	}
  7040	   112	
  7041	   113	/**
  7042	   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
  7043	   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
  7044	   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
  7045	   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
  7046	   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
  7047	   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
  7048	   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
  7049	   121	 */
  7050	   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
  7051	   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  7052	   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
  7053	   125	  const warnings = [];
  7054	   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  7055	   127	
  7056	   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
  7057	   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
  7058	   130	
  7059	   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
  7060	   132	
  7061	   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
  7062	   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
  7063	   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
  7064	   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
  7065	   137	  }
  7066	   138	
  7067	   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
  7068	   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
  7069	   141	
  7070	   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  7071	   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
  7072	   144	    for (const w of jw) {
  7073	   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
  7074	   146	    }
  7075	   147	    for (const e of entries) {
  7076	   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
  7077	   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
  7078	   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
  7079	   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
  7080	   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
  7081	   153	        continue;
  7082	   154	      }
  7083	   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
  7084	   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
  7085	   157	    }
  7086	   158	  }
  7087	   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
  7088	   160	}
  7089	   161	
  7090	   162	/**
  7091	   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
  7092	   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
  7093	   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
  7094	   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
  7095	   167	 */
  7096	   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
  7097	   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  7098	   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  7099	   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  7100	   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  7101	   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
  7102	   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
  7103	   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
  7104	   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
  7105	   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
  7106	   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
  7107	   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  7108	   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
  7109	   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
  7110	   182	    }
  7111	   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
  7112	   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
  7113	   185	  });
  7114	   186	}
  7115	   187	
  7116	   188	/**
  7117	   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
  7118	   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
  7119	   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
  7120	   192	 */
  7121	   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
  7122	   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
  7123	   195	}
  7124	   196	
  7125	   197	/**
  7126	   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
  7127	   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
  7128	   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
  7129	   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
  7130	   202	 */
  7131	   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
  7132	   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
  7133	   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
  7134	   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
  7135	   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
  7136	   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
  7137	   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
  7138	   210	      let toAppend = entry;
  7139	   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
  7140	   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
  7141	   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
  7142	   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
  7143	   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
  7144	   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
  7145	   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
  7146	   218	          }
  7147	   219	        }
  7148	   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
  7149

  7159	     7	/**
  7160	     8	 * Append one entry as a single JSON line.

  7163	    11	export function appendEntry(fs, path, obj) {
