  7165	    13	}
  7166	    14	
  7167	    15	/**
  7168	    16	 * Read every parseable entry. Never throws.
  7169	    17	 * Warnings: {line, kind: 'torn-tail'|'parse-error'|'seq-order', ...}.
  7170	    18	 * A non-monotonic numeric seq is flagged but the entry is kept — replay and
  7171	    19	 * audit must still see it.
  7172	    20	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
  7173	    21	 * @returns {{entries: any[], warnings: any[]}}
  7174	    22	 */
  7175	    23	export function readAllTolerant(fs, path) {
  7176	    24	  /** @type {any[]} */
  7177	    25	  const entries = [];
  7178	    26	  /** @type {any[]} */
  7179	    27	  const warnings = [];
  7180	    28	
  7181	    29	  let raw;
  7182	    30	  try {
  7183	    31	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  7184	    32	  } catch {
  7185	    33	    return { entries, warnings };
  7186	    34	  }
  7187	    35	  if (raw === '') return { entries, warnings };
  7188	    36	
  7189	    37	  const terminated = raw.endsWith('\n');
  7190	    38	  const lines = raw.split('\n');
  7191	    39	  if (terminated) lines.pop();
  7192	    40	
  7193	    41	  let prevSeq = null;
  7194	    42	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  7195	    43	    const lineNo = i + 1;
  7196	    44	    const isLast = i === lines.length - 1;
  7197	    45	    const torn = isLast && !terminated;
  7198	    46	    let parsed;
  7199	    47	    try {
  7200	    48	      parsed = JSON.parse(lines[i]);
  7201	    49	    } catch {
  7202	    50	      warnings.push(torn ? { line: lineNo, kind: 'torn-tail' } : { line: lineNo, kind: 'parse-error' });
  7203	    51	      continue;
  7204	    52	    }
  7205	    53	    if (typeof parsed?.seq === 'number') {
  7206	    54	      if (prevSeq !== null && parsed.seq <= prevSeq) {
  7207	    55	        warnings.push({ line: lineNo, kind: 'seq-order', prev: prevSeq, seq: parsed.seq });
  7208	    56	      }
  7209	    57	      prevSeq = prevSeq === null ? parsed.seq : Math.max(prevSeq, parsed.seq);
  7210	    58	    }
  7211	    59	    entries.push(parsed);
  7212	    60	  }
  7213	    61	  return { entries, warnings };
  7214	    62	}

  7651	     3	import { emitEnvelope, usageError, parseFlagsStrict, resolveRoot } from './shared.mjs';
  7652	     4	
  7653	     5	// Flags that consume a value — needed to split positionals from flag tokens
  7654	     6	// without mistaking a flag's value for a positional. Keep in sync with the
  7655	     7	// strict spec below plus the implied common string flag (--root).
  7656	     8	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root']);
  7657	     9	
  7658	    10	/**
  7659	    11	 * `baton loop <subcommand>` — the Layer-2 goal-loop surface. v1 subcommands:
  7660	    12	 * `init "<goal>"` (scaffold loop.json; two-phase, idempotent, dry-run-able).
  7661	    13	 * Supervisor-side by design: the BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD guard deliberately
  7662	    14	 * does NOT apply here.
  7663	    15	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
  7664	    16	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
  7665	    17	 */
  7666	    18	export async function cmdLoop(args, io) {
  7667	    19	  /** @type {string[]} */
  7668	    20	  const positionals = [];
  7669	    21	  /** @type {string[]} */
  7670	    22	  const flagTokens = [];
  7671	    23	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
  7672	    24	    const a = args[i];
  7673	    25	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
  7674	    26	      flagTokens.push(a);
  7675	    27	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
  7676	    28	        i += 1;
  7677	    29	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
  7678	    30	      }
  7679	    31	    } else {
  7680	    32	      positionals.push(a);
  7681	    33	    }
  7682	    34	  }
  7683	    35	
  7684	    36	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, { 'dry-run': 'boolean' });
  7685	    37	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'loop', parsed.error);
  7686	    38	  const flags = parsed.flags;
  7687	    39	
  7688	    40	  const sub = positionals[0];
  7689	    41	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton loop init "<goal>"');
  7690	    42	  if (sub !== 'init') return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: init)`);
  7691	    43	
  7692	    44	  const goal = positionals[1];
  7693	    45	  if (goal === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', 'loop init requires a goal — baton loop init "<goal>"');
  7694	    46	  if (positionals.length > 2) return usageError(io, flags, 'loop', `unexpected argument '${positionals[2]}'`);
  7695	    47	
  7696	    48	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
  7697	    49	  const path = `${root}/loop.json`;
  7698	    50	
  7699	    51	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ data, /** @type {string} */ human) => {
  7700	    52	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
  7701	    53	    else io.stdout.write(`${human}\n`);
  7702	    54	    return 0;
  7703	    55	  };
  7704	    56	
  7705	    57	  // Idempotent: an existing spec is the user's — never overwritten.
  7706	    58	  if (io.fs.existsSync(path)) {
  7707	    59	    return finish({ created: false, path }, `baton loop init: ${path} already exists — left untouched`);
  7708	    60	  }
  7709	    61	  if (flags['dry-run'] === true) {
  7710	    62	    return finish(
  7711	    63	      { created: false, path, dryRun: true },
  7712	    64	      `baton loop init (dry-run): would write ${path} — the default baton/loop@1 spec with goal "${goal}"`,
  7713	    65	    );
  7714	    66	  }
  7715	    67	  ensureDir(io.fs, root);
  7716	    68	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, path, defaultLoopSpec(goal));
  7717	    69	  return finish({ created: true, path }, `baton loop init: wrote ${path} (goal "${goal}") — edit constraints/smoke, then run baton loop run`);
  7718	    70	}

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/util/jsonl.mjs | sed -n '1,220p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Append-only JSONL journal IO, tolerant of the corruption modes a
     3	 * kill-mid-write can produce: a torn (unterminated) tail, a corrupt middle
     4	 * line, and out-of-order sequence numbers.
     5	 */
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * Append one entry as a single JSON line.
     9	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    10	 */
    11	export function appendEntry(fs, path, obj) {
    12	  fs.appendFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(obj) + '\n');
    13	}
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Read every parseable entry. Never throws.
    17	 * Warnings: {line, kind: 'torn-tail'|'parse-error'|'seq-order', ...}.
    18	 * A non-monotonic numeric seq is flagged but the entry is kept — replay and
    19	 * audit must still see it.
    20	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    21	 * @returns {{entries: any[], warnings: any[]}}
    22	 */
    23	export function readAllTolerant(fs, path) {
    24	  /** @type {any[]} */
    25	  const entries = [];
    26	  /** @type {any[]} */
    27	  const warnings = [];
    28	
    29	  let raw;
    30	  try {
    31	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    32	  } catch {
    33	    return { entries, warnings };
    34	  }
    35	  if (raw === '') return { entries, warnings };
    36	
    37	  const terminated = raw.endsWith('\n');
    38	  const lines = raw.split('\n');
    39	  if (terminated) lines.pop();
    40	
    41	  let prevSeq = null;
    42	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
    43	    const lineNo = i + 1;
    44	    const isLast = i === lines.length - 1;
    45	    const torn = isLast && !terminated;
    46	    let parsed;
    47	    try {
    48	      parsed = JSON.parse(lines[i]);
    49	    } catch {
    50	      warnings.push(torn ? { line: lineNo, kind: 'torn-tail' } : { line: lineNo, kind: 'parse-error' });
    51	      continue;
    52	    }
    53	    if (typeof parsed?.seq === 'number') {
    54	      if (prevSeq !== null && parsed.seq <= prevSeq) {
    55	        warnings.push({ line: lineNo, kind: 'seq-order', prev: prevSeq, seq: parsed.seq });
    56	      }
    57	      prevSeq = prevSeq === null ? parsed.seq : Math.max(prevSeq, parsed.seq);
    58	    }
    59	    entries.push(parsed);
    60	  }
    61	  return { entries, warnings };
    62	}
